  16 |     | /// @title Gnosis Safe - A multisignature wallet with support for confirmations using signed messages based on ERC191.
  17 |     | /// @author Stefan George - <stefan@gnosis.io>
  18 |     | /// @author Richard Meissner - <richard@gnosis.io>
  19 |     | contract GnosisSafe is
  20 |     |     EtherPaymentFallback,
  21 |     |     Singleton,
  22 |     |     ModuleManager,
  23 |     |     OwnerManager,
  24 |     |     SignatureDecoder,
  25 |     |     SecuredTokenTransfer,
  26 |     |     ISignatureValidatorConstants,
  27 |     |     FallbackManager,
  28 |     |     StorageAccessible,
  29 |     |     GuardManager

  31 |     |     using GnosisSafeMath for uint256;
  32 |     | 
  33 |     |     string public constant VERSION = "1.3.0";
  34 |     | 
  35 |     |     // keccak256(
  36 |     |     //     "EIP712Domain(uint256 chainId,address verifyingContract)"
  37 |     |     // );
  38 |     |     bytes32 private constant DOMAIN_SEPARATOR_TYPEHASH = 0x47e79534a245952e8b16893a336b85a3d9ea9fa8c573f3d803afb92a79469218;
  39 |     | 
  40 |     |     // keccak256(
  41 |     |     //     "SafeTx(address to,uint256 value,bytes data,uint8 operation,uint256 safeTxGas,uint256 baseGas,uint256 gasPrice,address gasToken,address refundReceiver,uint256 nonce)"
  42 |     |     // );
  43 |     |     bytes32 private constant SAFE_TX_TYPEHASH = 0xbb8310d486368db6bd6f849402fdd73ad53d316b5a4b2644ad6efe0f941286d8;
  44 |     | 
  45 |     |     event SafeSetup(address indexed initiator, address[] owners, uint256 threshold, address initializer, address fallbackHandler);
  46 |     |     event ApproveHash(bytes32 indexed approvedHash, address indexed owner);
  47 |     |     event SignMsg(bytes32 indexed msgHash);
  48 |     |     event ExecutionFailure(bytes32 txHash, uint256 payment);
  49 |     |     event ExecutionSuccess(bytes32 txHash, uint256 payment);
  50 |     | 
  51 |     |     uint256 public nonce;
  52 |     |     bytes32 private _deprecatedDomainSeparator;
  53 |     |     // Mapping to keep track of all message hashes that have been approve by ALL REQUIRED owners
  54 |     |     mapping(bytes32 => uint256) public signedMessages;
  55 |     |     // Mapping to keep track of all hashes (message or transaction) that have been approve by ANY owners
  56 |     |     mapping(address => mapping(bytes32 => uint256)) public approvedHashes;
  57 |     | 
  58 |     |     // This constructor ensures that this contract can only be used as a master copy for Proxy contracts
  59 |     |     constructor() {
  60 |     |         // By setting the threshold it is not possible to call setup anymore,
  61 |     |         // so we create a Safe with 0 owners and threshold 1.
  62 |     |         // This is an unusable Safe, perfect for the singleton
  63 |     |         threshold = 1;
  64 |     |     }
  65 |     | 
  66 |     |     /// @dev Setup function sets initial storage of contract.
  67 |     |     /// @param _owners List of Safe owners.
  68 |     |     /// @param _threshold Number of required confirmations for a Safe transaction.
  69 |     |     /// @param to Contract address for optional delegate call.
  70 |     |     /// @param data Data payload for optional delegate call.
  71 |     |     /// @param fallbackHandler Handler for fallback calls to this contract
  72 |     |     /// @param paymentToken Token that should be used for the payment (0 is ETH)
  73 |     |     /// @param payment Value that should be paid
  74 |     |     /// @param paymentReceiver Adddress that should receive the payment (or 0 if tx.origin)
  75 |     |     function setup(
  76 |     |         address[] calldata _owners,
  77 |     |         uint256 _threshold,
  78 |     |         address to,
  79 |     |         bytes calldata data,
  80 |     |         address fallbackHandler,
  81 |     |         address paymentToken,
  82 |     |         uint256 payment,
  83 |     |         address payable paymentReceiver
  84 |     |     ) external {
  85 |     |         // setupOwners checks if the Threshold is already set, therefore preventing that this method is called twice
  86 |     |         setupOwners(_owners, _threshold);
  87 |     |         if (fallbackHandler != address(0)) internalSetFallbackHandler(fallbackHandler);
  88 |     |         // As setupOwners can only be called if the contract has not been initialized we don't need a check for setupModules
  89 |     |         setupModules(to, data);
  90 |     | 
  91 |     |         if (payment > 0) {
  92 |     |             // To avoid running into issues with EIP-170 we reuse the handlePayment function (to avoid adjusting code of that has been verified we do not adjust the method itself)
  93 |     |             // baseGas = 0, gasPrice = 1 and gas = payment => amount = (payment + 0) * 1 = payment
  94 |     |             handlePayment(payment, 0, 1, paymentToken, paymentReceiver);
  95 |     |         }
  96 |     |         emit SafeSetup(msg.sender, _owners, _threshold, to, fallbackHandler);
  97 |     |     }
  98 |     | 
  99 |     |     /// @dev Allows to execute a Safe transaction confirmed by required number of owners and then pays the account that submitted the transaction.
 100 |     |     ///      Note: The fees are always transferred, even if the user transaction fails.
 101 |     |     /// @param to Destination address of Safe transaction.
 102 |     |     /// @param value Ether value of Safe transaction.
 103 |     |     /// @param data Data payload of Safe transaction.
 104 |     |     /// @param operation Operation type of Safe transaction.
 105 |     |     /// @param safeTxGas Gas that should be used for the Safe transaction.
 106 |     |     /// @param baseGas Gas costs that are independent of the transaction execution(e.g. base transaction fee, signature check, payment of the refund)
 107 |     |     /// @param gasPrice Gas price that should be used for the payment calculation.
 108 |     |     /// @param gasToken Token address (or 0 if ETH) that is used for the payment.
 109 |     |     /// @param refundReceiver Address of receiver of gas payment (or 0 if tx.origin).
 110 |     |     /// @param signatures Packed signature data ({bytes32 r}{bytes32 s}{uint8 v})
 111 |     |     function execTransaction(
 112 |     |         address to,
 113 |     |         uint256 value,
 114 |     |         bytes calldata data,
 115 |     |         Enum.Operation operation,
 116 |     |         uint256 safeTxGas,
 117 |     |         uint256 baseGas,
 118 |     |         uint256 gasPrice,
 119 |     |         address gasToken,
 120 |     |         address payable refundReceiver,
 121 |     |         bytes memory signatures
 122 |     |     ) public payable virtual returns (bool success) {
 123 |     |         bytes32 txHash;
 124 |     |         // Use scope here to limit variable lifetime and prevent `stack too deep` errors
 125 |     |         {
 126 |     |             bytes memory txHashData =
 127 |     |                 encodeTransactionData(
 128 |     |                     // Transaction info
 129 |     |                     to,
 130 |     |                     value,
 131 |     |                     data,
 132 |     |                     operation,
 133 |     |                     safeTxGas,
 134 |     |                     // Payment info
 135 |     |                     baseGas,
 136 |     |                     gasPrice,
 137 |     |                     gasToken,
 138 |     |                     refundReceiver,
 139 |     |                     // Signature info
 140 |     |                     nonce
 141 |     |                 );
 142 |     |             // Increase nonce and execute transaction.
 143 |     |             nonce++;
 144 |     |             txHash = keccak256(txHashData);
 145 |     |             checkSignatures(txHash, txHashData, signatures);
 146 |     |         }
 147 |     |         address guard = getGuard();
 148 |     |         {
 149 |     |             if (guard != address(0)) {
 150 |     |                 Guard(guard).checkTransaction(
 151 |     |                     // Transaction info
 152 |     |                     to,
 153 |     |                     value,
 154 |     |                     data,
 155 |     |                     operation,
 156 |     |                     safeTxGas,
 157 |     |                     // Payment info
 158 |     |                     baseGas,
 159 |     |                     gasPrice,
 160 |     |                     gasToken,
 161 |     |                     refundReceiver,
 162 |     |                     // Signature info
 163 |     |                     signatures,
 164 |     |                     msg.sender
 165 |     |                 );
 166 |     |             }
 167 |     |         }
 168 |     |         // We require some gas to emit the events (at least 2500) after the execution and some to perform code until the execution (500)
 169 |     |         // We also include the 1/64 in the check that is not send along with a call to counteract potential shortings because of EIP-150
 170 |     |         require(gasleft() >= ((safeTxGas * 64) / 63).max(safeTxGas + 2500) + 500, "GS010");
 171 |     |         // Use scope here to limit variable lifetime and prevent `stack too deep` errors
 172 |     |         {
 173 |     |             uint256 gasUsed = gasleft();
 174 |     |             // If the gasPrice is 0 we assume that nearly all available gas can be used (it is always more than safeTxGas)
 175 |     |             // We only substract 2500 (compared to the 3000 before) to ensure that the amount passed is still higher than safeTxGas
 176 |     |             success = execute(to, value, data, operation, gasPrice == 0 ? (gasleft() - 2500) : safeTxGas);
 177 |     |             gasUsed = gasUsed.sub(gasleft());
 178 |     |             // If no safeTxGas and no gasPrice was set (e.g. both are 0), then the internal tx is required to be successful
 179 |     |             // This makes it possible to use `estimateGas` without issues, as it searches for the minimum gas where the tx doesn't revert
 180 |     |             require(success || safeTxGas != 0 || gasPrice != 0, "GS013");
 181 |     |             // We transfer the calculated tx costs to the tx.origin to avoid sending it to intermediate contracts that have made calls
 182 |     |             uint256 payment = 0;
 183 |     |             if (gasPrice > 0) {
 184 |     |                 payment = handlePayment(gasUsed, baseGas, gasPrice, gasToken, refundReceiver);
 185 |     |             }
 186 |     |             if (success) emit ExecutionSuccess(txHash, payment);
 187 |     |             else emit ExecutionFailure(txHash, payment);
 188 |     |         }
 189 |     |         {
 190 |     |             if (guard != address(0)) {
 191 |     |                 Guard(guard).checkAfterExecution(txHash, success);
 192 |     |             }
 193 |     |         }
 194 |     |     }
 195 |     | 
 196 |     |     function handlePayment(
 197 |     |         uint256 gasUsed,
 198 |     |         uint256 baseGas,
 199 |     |         uint256 gasPrice,
 200 |     |         address gasToken,
 201 |     |         address payable refundReceiver
 202 |     |     ) private returns (uint256 payment) {
 203 |     |         // solhint-disable-next-line avoid-tx-origin
 204 |     |         address payable receiver = refundReceiver == address(0) ? payable(tx.origin) : refundReceiver;
 205 |     |         if (gasToken == address(0)) {
 206 |     |             // For ETH we will only adjust the gas price to not be higher than the actual used gas price
 207 |     |             payment = gasUsed.add(baseGas).mul(gasPrice < tx.gasprice ? gasPrice : tx.gasprice);
 208 |     |             require(receiver.send(payment), "GS011");
 209 |     |         } else {
 210 |     |             payment = gasUsed.add(baseGas).mul(gasPrice);
 211 |     |             require(transferToken(gasToken, receiver, payment), "GS012");
 212 |     |         }
 213 |     |     }
 214 |     | 
 215 |     |     /**
 216 |     |      * @dev Checks whether the signature provided is valid for the provided data, hash. Will revert otherwise.
 217 |     |      * @param dataHash Hash of the data (could be either a message hash or transaction hash)
 218 |     |      * @param data That should be signed (this is passed to an external validator contract)
 219 |     |      * @param signatures Signature data that should be verified. Can be ECDSA signature, contract signature (EIP-1271) or approved hash.
 220 |     |      */
 221 |     |     function checkSignatures(
 222 |     |         bytes32 dataHash,
 223 |     |         bytes memory data,
 224 |     |         bytes memory signatures
 225 |     |     ) public view {
 226 |     |         // Load threshold to avoid multiple storage loads
 227 |     |         uint256 _threshold = threshold;
 228 |     |         // Check that a threshold is set
 229 |     |         require(_threshold > 0, "GS001");
 230 |     |         checkNSignatures(dataHash, data, signatures, _threshold);
 231 |     |     }
 232 |     | 
 233 |     |     /**
 234 |     |      * @dev Checks whether the signature provided is valid for the provided data, hash. Will revert otherwise.
 235 |     |      * @param dataHash Hash of the data (could be either a message hash or transaction hash)
 236 |     |      * @param data That should be signed (this is passed to an external validator contract)
 237 |     |      * @param signatures Signature data that should be verified. Can be ECDSA signature, contract signature (EIP-1271) or approved hash.
 238 |     |      * @param requiredSignatures Amount of required valid signatures.
 239 |     |      */
 240 |     |     function checkNSignatures(
 241 |     |         bytes32 dataHash,
 242 |     |         bytes memory data,
 243 |     |         bytes memory signatures,
 244 |     |         uint256 requiredSignatures
 245 |     |     ) public view {
 246 |     |         // Check that the provided signature data is not too short
 247 |     |         require(signatures.length >= requiredSignatures.mul(65), "GS020");
 248 |     |         // There cannot be an owner with address 0.
 249 |     |         address lastOwner = address(0);
 250 |     |         address currentOwner;
 251 |     |         uint8 v;
 252 |     |         bytes32 r;
 253 |     |         bytes32 s;
 254 |     |         uint256 i;
 255 |     |         for (i = 0; i < requiredSignatures; i++) {
 256 |     |             (v, r, s) = signatureSplit(signatures, i);
 257 |     |             if (v == 0) {
 258 |     |                 // If v is 0 then it is a contract signature
 259 |     |                 // When handling contract signatures the address of the contract is encoded into r
 260 |     |                 currentOwner = address(uint160(uint256(r)));
 261 |     | 
 262 |     |                 // Check that signature data pointer (s) is not pointing inside the static part of the signatures bytes
 263 |     |                 // This check is not completely accurate, since it is possible that more signatures than the threshold are send.
 264 |     |                 // Here we only check that the pointer is not pointing inside the part that is being processed
 265 |     |                 require(uint256(s) >= requiredSignatures.mul(65), "GS021");
 266 |     | 
 267 |     |                 // Check that signature data pointer (s) is in bounds (points to the length of data -> 32 bytes)
 268 |     |                 require(uint256(s).add(32) <= signatures.length, "GS022");
 269 |     | 
 270 |     |                 // Check if the contract signature is in bounds: start of data is s + 32 and end is start + signature length
 271 |     |                 uint256 contractSignatureLen;
 272 |     |                 // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
 273 |     |                 assembly {
 274 |     |                     contractSignatureLen := mload(add(add(signatures, s), 0x20))
 275 |     |                 }
 276 |     |                 require(uint256(s).add(32).add(contractSignatureLen) <= signatures.length, "GS023");
 277 |     | 
 278 |     |                 // Check signature
 279 |     |                 bytes memory contractSignature;
 280 |     |                 // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
 281 |     |                 assembly {
 282 |     |                     // The signature data for contract signatures is appended to the concatenated signatures and the offset is stored in s
 283 |     |                     contractSignature := add(add(signatures, s), 0x20)
 284 |     |                 }
 285 |     |                 require(ISignatureValidator(currentOwner).isValidSignature(data, contractSignature) == EIP1271_MAGIC_VALUE, "GS024");
 286 |     |             } else if (v == 1) {
 287 |     |                 // If v is 1 then it is an approved hash
 288 |     |                 // When handling approved hashes the address of the approver is encoded into r
 289 |     |                 currentOwner = address(uint160(uint256(r)));
 290 |     |                 // Hashes are automatically approved by the sender of the message or when they have been pre-approved via a separate transaction
 291 |     |                 require(msg.sender == currentOwner || approvedHashes[currentOwner][dataHash] != 0, "GS025");
 292 |     |             } else if (v > 30) {
 293 |     |                 // If v > 30 then default va (27,28) has been adjusted for eth_sign flow
 294 |     |                 // To support eth_sign and similar we adjust v and hash the messageHash with the Ethereum message prefix before applying ecrecover
 295 |     |                 currentOwner = ecrecover(keccak256(abi.encodePacked("\x19Ethereum Signed Message:\n32", dataHash)), v - 4, r, s);
 296 |     |             } else {
 297 |     |                 // Default is the ecrecover flow with the provided data hash
 298 |     |                 // Use ecrecover with the messageHash for EOA signatures
 299 |     |                 currentOwner = ecrecover(dataHash, v, r, s);
 300 |     |             }
 301 |     |             require(currentOwner > lastOwner && owners[currentOwner] != address(0) && currentOwner != SENTINEL_OWNERS, "GS026");
 302 |     |             lastOwner = currentOwner;
 303 |     |         }
 304 |     |     }
 305 |     | 
 306 |     |     /// @dev Allows to estimate a Safe transaction.
 307 |     |     ///      This method is only meant for estimation purpose, therefore the call will always revert and encode the result in the revert data.
 308 |     |     ///      Since the `estimateGas` function includes refunds, call this method to get an estimated of the costs that are deducted from the safe with `execTransaction`
 309 |     |     /// @param to Destination address of Safe transaction.
 310 |     |     /// @param value Ether value of Safe transaction.
 311 |     |     /// @param data Data payload of Safe transaction.
 312 |     |     /// @param operation Operation type of Safe transaction.
 313 |     |     /// @return Estimate without refunds and overhead fees (base transaction and payload data gas costs).
 314 |     |     /// @notice Deprecated in favor of common/StorageAccessible.sol and will be removed in next version.
 315 |     |     function requiredTxGas(
 316 |     |         address to,
 317 |     |         uint256 value,
 318 |     |         bytes calldata data,
 319 |     |         Enum.Operation operation
 320 |     |     ) external returns (uint256) {
 321 |     |         uint256 startGas = gasleft();
 322 |     |         // We don't provide an error message here, as we use it to return the estimate
 323 |     |         require(execute(to, value, data, operation, gasleft()));
 324 |     |         uint256 requiredGas = startGas - gasleft();
 325 |     |         // Convert response to string and return via error message
 326 |     |         revert(string(abi.encodePacked(requiredGas)));
 327 |     |     }
 328 |     | 
 329 |     |     /**
 330 |     |      * @dev Marks a hash as approved. This can be used to validate a hash that is used by a signature.
 331 |     |      * @param hashToApprove The hash that should be marked as approved for signatures that are verified by this contract.
 332 |     |      */
 333 |     |     function approveHash(bytes32 hashToApprove) external {
 334 |     |         require(owners[msg.sender] != address(0), "GS030");
 335 |     |         approvedHashes[msg.sender][hashToApprove] = 1;
 336 |     |         emit ApproveHash(hashToApprove, msg.sender);
 337 |     |     }

 339 |     |     /// @dev Returns the chain id used by this contract.
 340 |     |     function getChainId() public view returns (uint256) {
 341 |     |         uint256 id;
 342 |     |         // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
 343 |     |         assembly {
 344 |     |             id := chainid()
 345 |     |         }
 346 |     |         return id;
 347 |     |     }
 348 |     | 
 349 |     |     function domainSeparator() public view returns (bytes32) {
 350 |     |         return keccak256(abi.encode(DOMAIN_SEPARATOR_TYPEHASH, getChainId(), this));
 351 |     |     }
 352 |     | 
 353 |     |     /// @dev Returns the bytes that are hashed to be signed by owners.
 354 |     |     /// @param to Destination address.
 355 |     |     /// @param value Ether value.
 356 |     |     /// @param data Data payload.
 357 |     |     /// @param operation Operation type.
 358 |     |     /// @param safeTxGas Gas that should be used for the safe transaction.
 359 |     |     /// @param baseGas Gas costs for that are independent of the transaction execution(e.g. base transaction fee, signature check, payment of the refund)
 360 |     |     /// @param gasPrice Maximum gas price that should be used for this transaction.
 361 |     |     /// @param gasToken Token address (or 0 if ETH) that is used for the payment.
 362 |     |     /// @param refundReceiver Address of receiver of gas payment (or 0 if tx.origin).
 363 |     |     /// @param _nonce Transaction nonce.
 364 |     |     /// @return Transaction hash bytes.
 365 |     |     function encodeTransactionData(
 366 |     |         address to,
 367 |     |         uint256 value,
 368 |     |         bytes calldata data,
 369 |     |         Enum.Operation operation,
 370 |     |         uint256 safeTxGas,
 371 |     |         uint256 baseGas,
 372 |     |         uint256 gasPrice,
 373 |     |         address gasToken,
 374 |     |         address refundReceiver,
 375 |     |         uint256 _nonce
 376 |     |     ) public view returns (bytes memory) {
 377 |     |         bytes32 safeTxHash =
 378 |     |             keccak256(
 379 |     |                 abi.encode(
 380 |     |                     SAFE_TX_TYPEHASH,
 381 |     |                     to,
 382 |     |                     value,
 383 |     |                     keccak256(data),
 384 |     |                     operation,
 385 |     |                     safeTxGas,
 386 |     |                     baseGas,
 387 |     |                     gasPrice,
 388 |     |                     gasToken,
 389 |     |                     refundReceiver,
 390 |     |                     _nonce
 391 |     |                 )
 392 |     |             );
 393 |     |         return abi.encodePacked(bytes1(0x19), bytes1(0x01), domainSeparator(), safeTxHash);
 394 |     |     }
 395 |     | 
 396 |     |     /// @dev Returns hash to be signed by owners.
 397 |     |     /// @param to Destination address.
 398 |     |     /// @param value Ether value.
 399 |     |     /// @param data Data payload.
 400 |     |     /// @param operation Operation type.
 401 |     |     /// @param safeTxGas Fas that should be used for the safe transaction.
 402 |     |     /// @param baseGas Gas costs for data used to trigger the safe transaction.
 403 |     |     /// @param gasPrice Maximum gas price that should be used for this transaction.
 404 |     |     /// @param gasToken Token address (or 0 if ETH) that is used for the payment.
 405 |     |     /// @param refundReceiver Address of receiver of gas payment (or 0 if tx.origin).
 406 |     |     /// @param _nonce Transaction nonce.
 407 |     |     /// @return Transaction hash.
 408 |     |     function getTransactionHash(
 409 |     |         address to,
 410 |     |         uint256 value,
 411 |     |         bytes calldata data,
 412 |     |         Enum.Operation operation,
 413 |     |         uint256 safeTxGas,
 414 |     |         uint256 baseGas,
 415 |     |         uint256 gasPrice,
 416 |     |         address gasToken,
 417 |     |         address refundReceiver,
 418 |     |         uint256 _nonce
 419 |     |     ) public view returns (bytes32) {
 420 |     |         return keccak256(encodeTransactionData(to, value, data, operation, safeTxGas, baseGas, gasPrice, gasToken, refundReceiver, _nonce));
 421 |     |     }

  4 |     | 
  5 |     | /// @title Executor - A contract that can execute transactions
  6 |     | /// @author Richard Meissner - <richard@gnosis.pm>
  7 |     | contract Executor {
  8 |     |     function execute(
  9 |     |         address to,
 10 |     |         uint256 value,
 11 |     |         bytes memory data,
 12 |     |         Enum.Operation operation,
 13 |     |         uint256 txGas
 14 |     |     ) internal returns (bool success) {
 15 |     |         if (operation == Enum.Operation.DelegateCall) {
 16 |     |             // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
 17 |     |             assembly {
 18 |     |                 success := delegatecall(txGas, to, add(data, 0x20), mload(data), 0, 0)
 19 |     |             }
 20 |     |         } else {
 21 |     |             // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
 22 |     |             assembly {
 23 |     |                 success := call(txGas, to, value, add(data, 0x20), mload(data), 0, 0)
 24 |     |             }
 25 |     |         }
 26 |     |     }
 27 |     | }

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@gnosis.pm/safe-contracts/contracts/base/FallbackManager.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
  2 |     | pragma solidity >=0.7.0 <0.9.0;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | import "../common/SelfAuthorized.sol";
  5 |     | 
  6 |     | /// @title Fallback Manager - A contract that manages fallback calls made to this contract
  7 |     | /// @author Richard Meissner - <richard@gnosis.pm>
  8 |     | contract FallbackManager is SelfAuthorized {
  9 |     |     event ChangedFallbackHandler(address handler);
 10 |     | 
 11 |     |     // keccak256("fallback_manager.handler.address")
 12 |     |     bytes32 internal constant FALLBACK_HANDLER_STORAGE_SLOT = 0x6c9a6c4a39284e37ed1cf53d337577d14212a4870fb976a4366c693b939918d5;
 13 |     | 
 14 |     |     function internalSetFallbackHandler(address handler) internal {
 15 |     |         bytes32 slot = FALLBACK_HANDLER_STORAGE_SLOT;
 16 |     |         // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
 17 |     |         assembly {
 18 |     |             sstore(slot, handler)
 19 |     |         }
 20 |     |     }
 21 |     | 
 22 |     |     /// @dev Allows to add a contract to handle fallback calls.
 23 |     |     ///      Only fallback calls without value and with data will be forwarded.
 24 |     |     ///      This can only be done via a Safe transaction.
 25 |     |     /// @param handler contract to handle fallbacks calls.
 26 |     |     function setFallbackHandler(address handler) public authorized {
 27 |     |         internalSetFallbackHandler(handler);
 28 |     |         emit ChangedFallbackHandler(handler);
 29 |     |     }
 30 |     | 
 31 |     |     // solhint-disable-next-line payable-fallback,no-complex-fallback
 32 |     |     fallback() external {
 33 |     |         bytes32 slot = FALLBACK_HANDLER_STORAGE_SLOT;
 34 |     |         // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
 35 |     |         assembly {
 36 |     |             let handler := sload(slot)
 37 |     |             if iszero(handler) {
 38 |     |                 return(0, 0)
 39 |     |             }
 40 |     |             calldatacopy(0, 0, calldatasize())
 41 |     |             // The msg.sender address is shifted to the left by 12 bytes to remove the padding
 42 |     |             // Then the address without padding is stored right after the calldata
 43 |     |             mstore(calldatasize(), shl(96, caller()))
 44 |     |             // Add 20 bytes for the address appended add the end
 45 |     |             let success := call(gas(), handler, 0, 0, add(calldatasize(), 20), 0, 0)
 46 |     |             returndatacopy(0, 0, returndatasize())
 47 |     |             if iszero(success) {
 48 |     |                 revert(0, returndatasize())
 49 |     |             }
 50 |     |             return(0, returndatasize())
 51 |     |         }
 52 |     |     }
 53 |     | }
 54 |     |

   3 |     | import "../common/Enum.sol";
   4 |     | import "../common/SelfAuthorized.sol";
   5 |     | import "./Executor.sol";
   6 |     | 
   7 |     | /// @title Module Manager - A contract that manages modules that can execute transactions via this contract
   8 |     | /// @author Stefan George - <stefan@gnosis.pm>
   9 |     | /// @author Richard Meissner - <richard@gnosis.pm>
  10 |     | contract ModuleManager is SelfAuthorized, Executor {
  11 |     |     event EnabledModule(address module);
  12 |     |     event DisabledModule(address module);
  13 |     |     event ExecutionFromModuleSuccess(address indexed module);
  14 |     |     event ExecutionFromModuleFailure(address indexed module);
  15 |     | 
  16 |     |     address internal constant SENTINEL_MODULES = address(0x1);
  17 |     | 
  18 |     |     mapping(address => address) internal modules;
  19 |     | 
  20 |     |     function setupModules(address to, bytes memory data) internal {
  21 |     |         require(modules[SENTINEL_MODULES] == address(0), "GS100");
  22 |     |         modules[SENTINEL_MODULES] = SENTINEL_MODULES;
  23 |     |         if (to != address(0))
  24 |     |             // Setup has to complete successfully or transaction fails.
  25 |     |             require(execute(to, 0, data, Enum.Operation.DelegateCall, gasleft()), "GS000");
  26 |     |     }
  27 |     | 
  28 |     |     /// @dev Allows to add a module to the whitelist.
  29 |     |     ///      This can only be done via a Safe transaction.
  30 |     |     /// @notice Enables the module `module` for the Safe.
  31 |     |     /// @param module Module to be whitelisted.
  32 |     |     function enableModule(address module) public authorized {
  33 |     |         // Module address cannot be null or sentinel.
  34 |     |         require(module != address(0) && module != SENTINEL_MODULES, "GS101");
  35 |     |         // Module cannot be added twice.
  36 |     |         require(modules[module] == address(0), "GS102");
  37 |     |         modules[module] = modules[SENTINEL_MODULES];
  38 |     |         modules[SENTINEL_MODULES] = module;
  39 |     |         emit EnabledModule(module);
  40 |     |     }
  41 |     | 
  42 |     |     /// @dev Allows to remove a module from the whitelist.
  43 |     |     ///      This can only be done via a Safe transaction.
  44 |     |     /// @notice Disables the module `module` for the Safe.
  45 |     |     /// @param prevModule Module that pointed to the module to be removed in the linked list
  46 |     |     /// @param module Module to be removed.
  47 |     |     function disableModule(address prevModule, address module) public authorized {
  48 |     |         // Validate module address and check that it corresponds to module index.
  49 |     |         require(module != address(0) && module != SENTINEL_MODULES, "GS101");
  50 |     |         require(modules[prevModule] == module, "GS103");
  51 |     |         modules[prevModule] = modules[module];
  52 |     |         modules[module] = address(0);
  53 |     |         emit DisabledModule(module);
  54 |     |     }
  55 |     | 
  56 |     |     /// @dev Allows a Module to execute a Safe transaction without any further confirmations.
  57 |     |     /// @param to Destination address of module transaction.
  58 |     |     /// @param value Ether value of module transaction.
  59 |     |     /// @param data Data payload of module transaction.
  60 |     |     /// @param operation Operation type of module transaction.
  61 |     |     function execTransactionFromModule(
  62 |     |         address to,
  63 |     |         uint256 value,
  64 |     |         bytes memory data,
  65 |     |         Enum.Operation operation
  66 |     |     ) public virtual returns (bool success) {
  67 |     |         // Only whitelisted modules are allowed.
  68 |     |         require(msg.sender != SENTINEL_MODULES && modules[msg.sender] != address(0), "GS104");
  69 |     |         // Execute transaction without further confirmations.
  70 |     |         success = execute(to, value, data, operation, gasleft());
  71 |     |         if (success) emit ExecutionFromModuleSuccess(msg.sender);
  72 |     |         else emit ExecutionFromModuleFailure(msg.sender);
  73 |     |     }
  74 |     | 
  75 |     |     /// @dev Allows a Module to execute a Safe transaction without any further confirmations and return data
  76 |     |     /// @param to Destination address of module transaction.
  77 |     |     /// @param value Ether value of module transaction.
  78 |     |     /// @param data Data payload of module transaction.
  79 |     |     /// @param operation Operation type of module transaction.
  80 |     |     function execTransactionFromModuleReturnData(
  81 |     |         address to,
  82 |     |         uint256 value,
  83 |     |         bytes memory data,
  84 |     |         Enum.Operation operation
  85 |     |     ) public returns (bool success, bytes memory returnData) {
  86 |     |         success = execTransactionFromModule(to, value, data, operation);
  87 |     |         // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
  88 |     |         assembly {
  89 |     |             // Load free memory location
  90 |     |             let ptr := mload(0x40)
  91 |     |             // We allocate memory for the return data by setting the free memory location to
  92 |     |             // current free memory location + data size + 32 bytes for data size value
  93 |     |             mstore(0x40, add(ptr, add(returndatasize(), 0x20)))
  94 |     |             // Store the size
  95 |     |             mstore(ptr, returndatasize())
  96 |     |             // Store the data
  97 |     |             returndatacopy(add(ptr, 0x20), 0, returndatasize())
  98 |     |             // Point the return data to the correct memory location
  99 |     |             returnData := ptr
 100 |     |         }
 101 |     |     }
 102 |     | 
 103 |     |     /// @dev Returns if an module is enabled
 104 |     |     /// @return True if the module is enabled
 105 |     |     function isModuleEnabled(address module) public view returns (bool) {
 106 |     |         return SENTINEL_MODULES != module && modules[module] != address(0);
 107 |     |     }
 108 |     | 
 109 |     |     /// @dev Returns array of modules.
 110 |     |     /// @param start Start of the page.
 111 |     |     /// @param pageSize Maximum number of modules that should be returned.
 112 |     |     /// @return array Array of modules.
 113 |     |     /// @return next Start of the next page.
 114 |     |     function getModulesPaginated(address start, uint256 pageSize) external view returns (address[] memory array, address next) {
 115 |     |         // Init array with max page size
 116 |     |         array = new address[](pageSize);
 117 |     | 
 118 |     |         // Populate return array
 119 |     |         uint256 moduleCount = 0;
 120 |     |         address currentModule = modules[start];
 121 |     |         while (currentModule != address(0x0) && currentModule != SENTINEL_MODULES && moduleCount < pageSize) {
 122 |     |             array[moduleCount] = currentModule;
 123 |     |             currentModule = modules[currentModule];
 124 |     |             moduleCount++;
 125 |     |         }
 126 |     |         next = currentModule;
 127 |     |         // Set correct size of returned array
 128 |     |         // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
 129 |     |         assembly {
 130 |     |             mstore(array, moduleCount)
 131 |     |         }
 132 |     |     }
 133 |     | }

   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
   2 |     | pragma solidity >=0.7.0 <0.9.0;
   3 |     | import "../common/SelfAuthorized.sol";
   4 |     | 
   5 |     | /// @title OwnerManager - Manages a set of owners and a threshold to perform actions.
   6 |     | /// @author Stefan George - <stefan@gnosis.pm>
   7 |     | /// @author Richard Meissner - <richard@gnosis.pm>
   8 |     | contract OwnerManager is SelfAuthorized {
   9 |     |     event AddedOwner(address owner);
  10 |     |     event RemovedOwner(address owner);
  11 |     |     event ChangedThreshold(uint256 threshold);
  12 |     | 
  13 |     |     address internal constant SENTINEL_OWNERS = address(0x1);
  14 |     | 
  15 |     |     mapping(address => address) internal owners;
  16 |     |     uint256 internal ownerCount;
  17 |     |     uint256 internal threshold;
  18 |     | 
  19 |     |     /// @dev Setup function sets initial storage of contract.
  20 |     |     /// @param _owners List of Safe owners.
  21 |     |     /// @param _threshold Number of required confirmations for a Safe transaction.
  22 |     |     function setupOwners(address[] memory _owners, uint256 _threshold) internal {
  23 |     |         // Threshold can only be 0 at initialization.
  24 |     |         // Check ensures that setup function can only be called once.
  25 |     |         require(threshold == 0, "GS200");
  26 |     |         // Validate that threshold is smaller than number of added owners.
  27 |     |         require(_threshold <= _owners.length, "GS201");
  28 |     |         // There has to be at least one Safe owner.
  29 |     |         require(_threshold >= 1, "GS202");
  30 |     |         // Initializing Safe owners.
  31 |     |         address currentOwner = SENTINEL_OWNERS;
  32 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < _owners.length; i++) {
  33 |     |             // Owner address cannot be null.
  34 |     |             address owner = _owners[i];
  35 |     |             require(owner != address(0) && owner != SENTINEL_OWNERS && owner != address(this) && currentOwner != owner, "GS203");
  36 |     |             // No duplicate owners allowed.
  37 |     |             require(owners[owner] == address(0), "GS204");
  38 |     |             owners[currentOwner] = owner;
  39 |     |             currentOwner = owner;
  40 |     |         }
  41 |     |         owners[currentOwner] = SENTINEL_OWNERS;
  42 |     |         ownerCount = _owners.length;
  43 |     |         threshold = _threshold;
  44 |     |     }
  45 |     | 
  46 |     |     /// @dev Allows to add a new owner to the Safe and update the threshold at the same time.
  47 |     |     ///      This can only be done via a Safe transaction.
  48 |     |     /// @notice Adds the owner `owner` to the Safe and updates the threshold to `_threshold`.
  49 |     |     /// @param owner New owner address.
  50 |     |     /// @param _threshold New threshold.
  51 |     |     function addOwnerWithThreshold(address owner, uint256 _threshold) public authorized {
  52 |     |         // Owner address cannot be null, the sentinel or the Safe itself.
  53 |     |         require(owner != address(0) && owner != SENTINEL_OWNERS && owner != address(this), "GS203");
  54 |     |         // No duplicate owners allowed.
  55 |     |         require(owners[owner] == address(0), "GS204");
  56 |     |         owners[owner] = owners[SENTINEL_OWNERS];
  57 |     |         owners[SENTINEL_OWNERS] = owner;
  58 |     |         ownerCount++;
  59 |     |         emit AddedOwner(owner);
  60 |     |         // Change threshold if threshold was changed.
  61 |     |         if (threshold != _threshold) changeThreshold(_threshold);
  62 |     |     }
  63 |     | 
  64 |     |     /// @dev Allows to remove an owner from the Safe and update the threshold at the same time.
  65 |     |     ///      This can only be done via a Safe transaction.
  66 |     |     /// @notice Removes the owner `owner` from the Safe and updates the threshold to `_threshold`.
  67 |     |     /// @param prevOwner Owner that pointed to the owner to be removed in the linked list
  68 |     |     /// @param owner Owner address to be removed.
  69 |     |     /// @param _threshold New threshold.
  70 |     |     function removeOwner(
  71 |     |         address prevOwner,
  72 |     |         address owner,
  73 |     |         uint256 _threshold
  74 |     |     ) public authorized {
  75 |     |         // Only allow to remove an owner, if threshold can still be reached.
  76 |     |         require(ownerCount - 1 >= _threshold, "GS201");
  77 |     |         // Validate owner address and check that it corresponds to owner index.
  78 |     |         require(owner != address(0) && owner != SENTINEL_OWNERS, "GS203");
  79 |     |         require(owners[prevOwner] == owner, "GS205");
  80 |     |         owners[prevOwner] = owners[owner];
  81 |     |         owners[owner] = address(0);
  82 |     |         ownerCount--;
  83 |     |         emit RemovedOwner(owner);
  84 |     |         // Change threshold if threshold was changed.
  85 |     |         if (threshold != _threshold) changeThreshold(_threshold);
  86 |     |     }
  87 |     | 
  88 |     |     /// @dev Allows to swap/replace an owner from the Safe with another address.
  89 |     |     ///      This can only be done via a Safe transaction.
  90 |     |     /// @notice Replaces the owner `oldOwner` in the Safe with `newOwner`.
  91 |     |     /// @param prevOwner Owner that pointed to the owner to be replaced in the linked list
  92 |     |     /// @param oldOwner Owner address to be replaced.
  93 |     |     /// @param newOwner New owner address.
  94 |     |     function swapOwner(
  95 |     |         address prevOwner,
  96 |     |         address oldOwner,
  97 |     |         address newOwner
  98 |     |     ) public authorized {
  99 |     |         // Owner address cannot be null, the sentinel or the Safe itself.
 100 |     |         require(newOwner != address(0) && newOwner != SENTINEL_OWNERS && newOwner != address(this), "GS203");
 101 |     |         // No duplicate owners allowed.
 102 |     |         require(owners[newOwner] == address(0), "GS204");
 103 |     |         // Validate oldOwner address and check that it corresponds to owner index.
 104 |     |         require(oldOwner != address(0) && oldOwner != SENTINEL_OWNERS, "GS203");
 105 |     |         require(owners[prevOwner] == oldOwner, "GS205");
 106 |     |         owners[newOwner] = owners[oldOwner];
 107 |     |         owners[prevOwner] = newOwner;
 108 |     |         owners[oldOwner] = address(0);
 109 |     |         emit RemovedOwner(oldOwner);
 110 |     |         emit AddedOwner(newOwner);
 111 |     |     }
 112 |     | 
 113 |     |     /// @dev Allows to update the number of required confirmations by Safe owners.
 114 |     |     ///      This can only be done via a Safe transaction.
 115 |     |     /// @notice Changes the threshold of the Safe to `_threshold`.
 116 |     |     /// @param _threshold New threshold.
 117 |     |     function changeThreshold(uint256 _threshold) public authorized {
 118 |     |         // Validate that threshold is smaller than number of owners.
 119 |     |         require(_threshold <= ownerCount, "GS201");
 120 |     |         // There has to be at least one Safe owner.
 121 |     |         require(_threshold >= 1, "GS202");
 122 |     |         threshold = _threshold;
 123 |     |         emit ChangedThreshold(threshold);
 124 |     |     }
 125 |     | 
 126 |     |     function getThreshold() public view returns (uint256) {
 127 |     |         return threshold;
 128 |     |     }
 129 |     | 
 130 |     |     function isOwner(address owner) public view returns (bool) {
 131 |     |         return owner != SENTINEL_OWNERS && owners[owner] != address(0);
 132 |     |     }
 133 |     | 
 134 |     |     /// @dev Returns array of owners.
 135 |     |     /// @return Array of Safe owners.
 136 |     |     function getOwners() public view returns (address[] memory) {
 137 |     |         address[] memory array = new address[](ownerCount);
 138 |     | 
 139 |     |         // populate return array
 140 |     |         uint256 index = 0;
 141 |     |         address currentOwner = owners[SENTINEL_OWNERS];
 142 |     |         while (currentOwner != SENTINEL_OWNERS) {
 143 |     |             array[index] = currentOwner;
 144 |     |             currentOwner = owners[currentOwner];
 145 |     |             index++;
 146 |     |         }
 147 |     |         return array;
 148 |     |     }
 149 |     | }

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@gnosis.pm/safe-contracts/contracts/common/SecuredTokenTransfer.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
  2 |     | pragma solidity >=0.7.0 <0.9.0;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | /// @title SecuredTokenTransfer - Secure token transfer
  5 |     | /// @author Richard Meissner - <richard@gnosis.pm>
  6 |     | contract SecuredTokenTransfer {
  7 |     |     /// @dev Transfers a token and returns if it was a success
  8 |     |     /// @param token Token that should be transferred
  9 |     |     /// @param receiver Receiver to whom the token should be transferred
 10 |     |     /// @param amount The amount of tokens that should be transferred
 11 |     |     function transferToken(
 12 |     |         address token,
 13 |     |         address receiver,
 14 |     |         uint256 amount
 15 |     |     ) internal returns (bool transferred) {
 16 |     |         // 0xa9059cbb - keccack("transfer(address,uint256)")
 17 |     |         bytes memory data = abi.encodeWithSelector(0xa9059cbb, receiver, amount);
 18 |     |         // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
 19 |     |         assembly {
 20 |     |             // We write the return value to scratch space.
 21 |     |             // See https://docs.soliditylang.org/en/v0.7.6/internals/layout_in_memory.html#layout-in-memory
 22 |     |             let success := call(sub(gas(), 10000), token, 0, add(data, 0x20), mload(data), 0, 0x20)
 23 |     |             switch returndatasize()
 24 |     |                 case 0 {
 25 |     |                     transferred := success
 26 |     |                 }
 27 |     |                 case 0x20 {
 28 |     |                     transferred := iszero(or(iszero(success), iszero(mload(0))))
 29 |     |                 }
 30 |     |                 default {
 31 |     |                     transferred := 0
 32 |     |                 }
 33 |     |         }
 34 |     |     }
 35 |     | }
 36 |     | 

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@gnosis.pm/safe-contracts/contracts/common/SelfAuthorized.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
  2 |     | pragma solidity >=0.7.0 <0.9.0;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | /// @title SelfAuthorized - authorizes current contract to perform actions
  5 |     | /// @author Richard Meissner - <richard@gnosis.pm>
  6 |     | contract SelfAuthorized {
  7 |     |     function requireSelfCall() private view {
  8 |     |         require(msg.sender == address(this), "GS031");
  9 |     |     }
 10 |     | 
 11 |     |     modifier authorized() {
 12 |     |         // This is a function call as it minimized the bytecode size
 13 |     |         requireSelfCall();
 14 |     |         _;
 15 |     |     }
 16 |     | }
 17 |     | 

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@gnosis.pm/safe-contracts/contracts/common/SignatureDecoder.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
  2 |     | pragma solidity >=0.7.0 <0.9.0;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | /// @title SignatureDecoder - Decodes signatures that a encoded as bytes
  5 |     | /// @author Richard Meissner - <richard@gnosis.pm>
  6 |     | contract SignatureDecoder {
  7 |     |     /// @dev divides bytes signature into `uint8 v, bytes32 r, bytes32 s`.
  8 |     |     /// @notice Make sure to peform a bounds check for @param pos, to avoid out of bounds access on @param signatures
  9 |     |     /// @param pos which signature to read. A prior bounds check of this parameter should be performed, to avoid out of bounds access
 10 |     |     /// @param signatures concatenated rsv signatures
 11 |     |     function signatureSplit(bytes memory signatures, uint256 pos)
 12 |     |         internal
 13 |     |         pure
 14 |     |         returns (
 15 |     |             uint8 v,
 16 |     |             bytes32 r,
 17 |     |             bytes32 s
 18 |     |         )
 19 |     |     {
 20 |     |         // The signature format is a compact form of:
 21 |     |         //   {bytes32 r}{bytes32 s}{uint8 v}
 22 |     |         // Compact means, uint8 is not padded to 32 bytes.
 23 |     |         // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
 24 |     |         assembly {
 25 |     |             let signaturePos := mul(0x41, pos)
 26 |     |             r := mload(add(signatures, add(signaturePos, 0x20)))
 27 |     |             s := mload(add(signatures, add(signaturePos, 0x40)))
 28 |     |             // Here we are loading the last 32 bytes, including 31 bytes
 29 |     |             // of 's'. There is no 'mload8' to do this.
 30 |     |             //
 31 |     |             // 'byte' is not working due to the Solidity parser, so lets
 32 |     |             // use the second best option, 'and'
 33 |     |             v := and(mload(add(signatures, add(signaturePos, 0x41))), 0xff)
 34 |     |         }
 35 |     |     }
 36 |     | }
 37 |     |

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@gnosis.pm/safe-contracts/contracts/common/StorageAccessible.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
  2 |     | pragma solidity >=0.7.0 <0.9.0;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | /// @title StorageAccessible - generic base contract that allows callers to access all internal storage.
  5 |     | /// @notice See https://github.com/gnosis/util-contracts/blob/bb5fe5fb5df6d8400998094fb1b32a178a47c3a1/contracts/StorageAccessible.sol
  6 |     | contract StorageAccessible {
  7 |     |     /**
  8 |     |      * @dev Reads `length` bytes of storage in the currents contract
  9 |     |      * @param offset - the offset in the current contract's storage in words to start reading from
 10 |     |      * @param length - the number of words (32 bytes) of data to read
 11 |     |      * @return the bytes that were read.
 12 |     |      */
 13 |     |     function getStorageAt(uint256 offset, uint256 length) public view returns (bytes memory) {
 14 |     |         bytes memory result = new bytes(length * 32);
 15 |     |         for (uint256 index = 0; index < length; index++) {
 16 |     |             // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
 17 |     |             assembly {
 18 |     |                 let word := sload(add(offset, index))
 19 |     |                 mstore(add(add(result, 0x20), mul(index, 0x20)), word)
 20 |     |             }
 21 |     |         }
 22 |     |         return result;
 23 |     |     }
 24 |     | 
 25 |     |     /**
 26 |     |      * @dev Performs a delegetecall on a targetContract in the context of self.
 27 |     |      * Internally reverts execution to avoid side effects (making it static).
 28 |     |      *
 29 |     |      * This method reverts with data equal to `abi.encode(bool(success), bytes(response))`.
 30 |     |      * Specifically, the `returndata` after a call to this method will be:
 31 |     |      * `success:bool || response.length:uint256 || response:bytes`.
 32 |     |      *
 33 |     |      * @param targetContract Address of the contract containing the code to execute.
 34 |     |      * @param calldataPayload Calldata that should be sent to the target contract (encoded method name and arguments).
 35 |     |      */
 36 |     |     function simulateAndRevert(address targetContract, bytes memory calldataPayload) external {
 37 |     |         // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
 38 |     |         assembly {
 39 |     |             let success := delegatecall(gas(), targetContract, add(calldataPayload, 0x20), mload(calldataPayload), 0, 0)
 40 |     | 
 41 |     |             mstore(0x00, success)
 42 |     |             mstore(0x20, returndatasize())
 43 |     |             returndatacopy(0x40, 0, returndatasize())
 44 |     |             revert(0, add(returndatasize(), 0x40))
 45 |     |         }
 46 |     |     }
 47 |     | }
 48 |     | 

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@gnosis.pm/safe-contracts/contracts/external/GnosisSafeMath.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
  2 |     | pragma solidity >=0.7.0 <0.9.0;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | /**
  5 |     |  * @title GnosisSafeMath
  6 |     |  * @dev Math operations with safety checks that revert on error
  7 |     |  * Renamed from SafeMath to GnosisSafeMath to avoid conflicts
  8 |     |  * TODO: remove once open zeppelin update to solc 0.5.0
  9 |     |  */
 10 |     | library GnosisSafeMath {
 11 |     |     /**
 12 |     |      * @dev Multiplies two numbers, reverts on overflow.
 13 |     |      */
 14 |     |     function mul(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {
 15 |     |         // Gas optimization: this is cheaper than requiring 'a' not being zero, but the
 16 |     |         // benefit is lost if 'b' is also tested.
 17 |     |         // See: https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-solidity/pull/522
 18 |     |         if (a == 0) {
 19 |     |             return 0;
 20 |     |         }
 21 |     | 
 22 |     |         uint256 c = a * b;
 23 |     |         require(c / a == b);
 24 |     | 
 25 |     |         return c;
 26 |     |     }
 27 |     | 
 28 |     |     /**
 29 |     |      * @dev Subtracts two numbers, reverts on overflow (i.e. if subtrahend is greater than minuend).
 30 |     |      */
 31 |     |     function sub(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {
 32 |     |         require(b <= a);
 33 |     |         uint256 c = a - b;
 34 |     | 
 35 |     |         return c;
 36 |     |     }
 37 |     | 
 38 |     |     /**
 39 |     |      * @dev Adds two numbers, reverts on overflow.
 40 |     |      */
 41 |     |     function add(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {
 42 |     |         uint256 c = a + b;
 43 |     |         require(c >= a);
 44 |     | 
 45 |     |         return c;
 46 |     |     }
 47 |     | 
 48 |     |     /**
 49 |     |      * @dev Returns the largest of two numbers.
 50 |     |      */
 51 |     |     function max(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {
 52 |     |         return a >= b ? a : b;
 53 |     |     }
 54 |     | }
 55 |     |

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@gnosis.pm/safe-contracts/contracts/proxies/GnosisSafeProxy.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
  2 |     | pragma solidity >=0.7.0 <0.9.0;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | /// @title IProxy - Helper interface to access masterCopy of the Proxy on-chain
  5 |     | /// @author Richard Meissner - <richard@gnosis.io>
  6 |     | interface IProxy {
  7 |     |     function masterCopy() external view returns (address);
  8 |     | }
  9 |     | 
 10 |     | /// @title GnosisSafeProxy - Generic proxy contract allows to execute all transactions applying the code of a master contract.
 11 |     | /// @author Stefan George - <stefan@gnosis.io>
 12 |     | /// @author Richard Meissner - <richard@gnosis.io>
 13 |     | contract GnosisSafeProxy {
 14 |     |     // singleton always needs to be first declared variable, to ensure that it is at the same location in the contracts to which calls are delegated.
 15 |     |     // To reduce deployment costs this variable is internal and needs to be retrieved via `getStorageAt`
 16 |     |     address internal singleton;
 17 |     | 
 18 |     |     /// @dev Constructor function sets address of singleton contract.
 19 |     |     /// @param _singleton Singleton address.
 20 |     |     constructor(address _singleton) {
 21 |     |         require(_singleton != address(0), "Invalid singleton address provided");
 22 |     |         singleton = _singleton;
 23 |     |     }
 24 |     | 
 25 |     |     /// @dev Fallback function forwards all transactions and returns all received return data.
 26 |     |     fallback() external payable {
 27 |     |         // solhint-disable-next-line no-inline-assembly
 28 |     |         assembly {
 29 |     |             let _singleton := and(sload(0), 0xffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffff)
 30 |     |             // 0xa619486e == keccak("masterCopy()"). The value is right padded to 32-bytes with 0s
 31 |     |             if eq(calldataload(0), 0xa619486e00000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000) {
 32 |     |                 mstore(0, _singleton)
 33 |     |                 return(0, 0x20)
 34 |     |             }
 35 |     |             calldatacopy(0, 0, calldatasize())
 36 |     |             let success := delegatecall(gas(), _singleton, 0, calldatasize(), 0, 0)
 37 |     |             returndatacopy(0, 0, returndatasize())
 38 |     |             if eq(success, 0) {
 39 |     |                 revert(0, returndatasize())
 40 |     |             }
 41 |     |             return(0, returndatasize())
 42 |     |         }
 43 |     |     }
 44 |     | }
 45 |     |

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/access/OwnableUpgradeable.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
  2 |     | 
  3 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
  4 |     | 
  5 |     | import "../utils/ContextUpgradeable.sol";
  6 |     | import "../proxy/utils/Initializable.sol";
  7 |     | 
  8 |     | /**
  9 |     |  * @dev Contract module which provides a basic access control mechanism, where
 10 |     |  * there is an account (an owner) that can be granted exclusive access to
 11 |     |  * specific functions.
 12 |     |  *
 13 |     |  * By default, the owner account will be the one that deploys the contract. This
 14 |     |  * can later be changed with {transferOwnership}.
 15 |     |  *
 16 |     |  * This module is used through inheritance. It will make available the modifier
 17 |     |  * `onlyOwner`, which can be applied to your functions to restrict their use to
 18 |     |  * the owner.
 19 |     |  */
 20 |     | abstract contract OwnableUpgradeable is Initializable, ContextUpgradeable {
 21 |     |     address private _owner;
 22 |     | 
 23 |     |     event OwnershipTransferred(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);
 24 |     | 
 25 |     |     /**
 26 |     |      * @dev Initializes the contract setting the deployer as the initial owner.
 27 |     |      */
 28 |     |     function __Ownable_init() internal initializer {
 29 |     |         __Context_init_unchained();
 30 |     |         __Ownable_init_unchained();
 31 |     |     }
 32 |     | 
 33 |     |     function __Ownable_init_unchained() internal initializer {
 34 |     |         _setOwner(_msgSender());
 35 |     |     }
 36 |     | 
 37 |     |     /**
 38 |     |      * @dev Returns the address of the current owner.
 39 |     |      */
 40 |     |     function owner() public view virtual returns (address) {
 41 |     |         return _owner;
 42 |     |     }
 43 |     | 
 44 |     |     /**
 45 |     |      * @dev Throws if called by any account other than the owner.
 46 |     |      */
 47 |     |     modifier onlyOwner() {
 48 |     |         require(owner() == _msgSender(), "Ownable: caller is not the owner");
 49 |     |         _;
 50 |     |     }
 51 |     | 
 52 |     |     /**
 53 |     |      * @dev Leaves the contract without owner. It will not be possible to call
 54 |     |      * `onlyOwner` functions anymore. Can only be called by the current owner.
 55 |     |      *
 56 |     |      * NOTE: Renouncing ownership will leave the contract without an owner,
 57 |     |      * thereby removing any functionality that is only available to the owner.
 58 |     |      */
 59 |     |     function renounceOwnership() public virtual onlyOwner {
 60 |     |         _setOwner(address(0));
 61 |     |     }
 62 |     | 
 63 |     |     /**
 64 |     |      * @dev Transfers ownership of the contract to a new account (`newOwner`).
 65 |     |      * Can only be called by the current owner.
 66 |     |      */
 67 |     |     function transferOwnership(address newOwner) public virtual onlyOwner {
 68 |     |         require(newOwner != address(0), "Ownable: new owner is the zero address");
 69 |     |         _setOwner(newOwner);
 70 |     |     }
 71 |     | 
 72 |     |     function _setOwner(address newOwner) private {
 73 |     |         address oldOwner = _owner;
 74 |     |         _owner = newOwner;
 75 |     |         emit OwnershipTransferred(oldOwner, newOwner);
 76 |     |     }
 77 |     |     uint256[49] private __gap;
 78 |     | }

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/proxy/ERC1967/ERC1967UpgradeUpgradeable.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2 |     | 
   3 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.2;
   4 |     | 
   5 |     | import "../beacon/IBeaconUpgradeable.sol";
   6 |     | import "../../utils/AddressUpgradeable.sol";
   7 |     | import "../../utils/StorageSlotUpgradeable.sol";
   8 |     | import "../utils/Initializable.sol";
   9 |     | 
  10 |     | /**
  11 |     |  * @dev This abstract contract provides getters and event emitting update functions for
  12 |     |  * https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-1967[EIP1967] slots.
  13 |     |  *
  14 |     |  * _Available since v4.1._
  15 |     |  *
  16 |     |  * @custom:oz-upgrades-unsafe-allow delegatecall
  17 |     |  */
  18 |     | abstract contract ERC1967UpgradeUpgradeable is Initializable {
  19 |     |     function __ERC1967Upgrade_init() internal initializer {
  20 |     |         __ERC1967Upgrade_init_unchained();
  21 |     |     }
  22 |     | 
  23 |     |     function __ERC1967Upgrade_init_unchained() internal initializer {
  24 |     |     }
  25 |     |     // This is the keccak-256 hash of "eip1967.proxy.rollback" subtracted by 1
  26 |     |     bytes32 private constant _ROLLBACK_SLOT = 0x4910fdfa16fed3260ed0e7147f7cc6da11a60208b5b9406d12a635614ffd9143;
  27 |     | 
  28 |     |     /**
  29 |     |      * @dev Storage slot with the address of the current implementation.
  30 |     |      * This is the keccak-256 hash of "eip1967.proxy.implementation" subtracted by 1, and is
  31 |     |      * validated in the constructor.
  32 |     |      */
  33 |     |     bytes32 internal constant _IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT = 0x360894a13ba1a3210667c828492db98dca3e2076cc3735a920a3ca505d382bbc;
  34 |     | 
  35 |     |     /**
  36 |     |      * @dev Emitted when the implementation is upgraded.
  37 |     |      */
  38 |     |     event Upgraded(address indexed implementation);
  39 |     | 
  40 |     |     /**
  41 |     |      * @dev Returns the current implementation address.
  42 |     |      */
  43 |     |     function _getImplementation() internal view returns (address) {
  44 |     |         return StorageSlotUpgradeable.getAddressSlot(_IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT).value;
  45 |     |     }
  46 |     | 
  47 |     |     /**
  48 |     |      * @dev Stores a new address in the EIP1967 implementation slot.
  49 |     |      */
  50 |     |     function _setImplementation(address newImplementation) private {
  51 |     |         require(AddressUpgradeable.isContract(newImplementation), "ERC1967: new implementation is not a contract");
  52 |     |         StorageSlotUpgradeable.getAddressSlot(_IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT).value = newImplementation;
  53 |     |     }
  54 |     | 
  55 |     |     /**
  56 |     |      * @dev Perform implementation upgrade
  57 |     |      *
  58 |     |      * Emits an {Upgraded} event.
  59 |     |      */
  60 |     |     function _upgradeTo(address newImplementation) internal {
  61 |     |         _setImplementation(newImplementation);
  62 |     |         emit Upgraded(newImplementation);
  63 |     |     }
  64 |     | 
  65 |     |     /**
  66 |     |      * @dev Perform implementation upgrade with additional setup call.
  67 |     |      *
  68 |     |      * Emits an {Upgraded} event.
  69 |     |      */
  70 |     |     function _upgradeToAndCall(
  71 |     |         address newImplementation,
  72 |     |         bytes memory data,
  73 |     |         bool forceCall
  74 |     |     ) internal {
  75 |     |         _upgradeTo(newImplementation);
  76 |     |         if (data.length > 0 || forceCall) {
  77 |     |             _functionDelegateCall(newImplementation, data);
  78 |     |         }
  79 |     |     }
  80 |     | 
  81 |     |     /**
  82 |     |      * @dev Perform implementation upgrade with security checks for UUPS proxies, and additional setup call.
  83 |     |      *
  84 |     |      * Emits an {Upgraded} event.
  85 |     |      */
  86 |     |     function _upgradeToAndCallSecure(
  87 |     |         address newImplementation,
  88 |     |         bytes memory data,
  89 |     |         bool forceCall
  90 |     |     ) internal {
  91 |     |         address oldImplementation = _getImplementation();
  92 |     | 
  93 |     |         // Initial upgrade and setup call
  94 |     |         _setImplementation(newImplementation);
  95 |     |         if (data.length > 0 || forceCall) {
  96 |     |             _functionDelegateCall(newImplementation, data);
  97 |     |         }
  98 |     | 
  99 |     |         // Perform rollback test if not already in progress
 100 |     |         StorageSlotUpgradeable.BooleanSlot storage rollbackTesting = StorageSlotUpgradeable.getBooleanSlot(_ROLLBACK_SLOT);
 101 |     |         if (!rollbackTesting.value) {
 102 |     |             // Trigger rollback using upgradeTo from the new implementation
 103 |     |             rollbackTesting.value = true;
 104 |     |             _functionDelegateCall(
 105 |     |                 newImplementation,
 106 |     |                 abi.encodeWithSignature("upgradeTo(address)", oldImplementation)
 107 |     |             );
 108 |     |             rollbackTesting.value = false;
 109 |     |             // Check rollback was effective
 110 |     |             require(oldImplementation == _getImplementation(), "ERC1967Upgrade: upgrade breaks further upgrades");
 111 |     |             // Finally reset to the new implementation and log the upgrade
 112 |     |             _upgradeTo(newImplementation);
 113 |     |         }
 114 |     |     }
 115 |     | 
 116 |     |     /**
 117 |     |      * @dev Storage slot with the admin of the contract.
 118 |     |      * This is the keccak-256 hash of "eip1967.proxy.admin" subtracted by 1, and is
 119 |     |      * validated in the constructor.
 120 |     |      */
 121 |     |     bytes32 internal constant _ADMIN_SLOT = 0xb53127684a568b3173ae13b9f8a6016e243e63b6e8ee1178d6a717850b5d6103;
 122 |     | 
 123 |     |     /**
 124 |     |      * @dev Emitted when the admin account has changed.
 125 |     |      */
 126 |     |     event AdminChanged(address previousAdmin, address newAdmin);
 127 |     | 
 128 |     |     /**
 129 |     |      * @dev Returns the current admin.
 130 |     |      */
 131 |     |     function _getAdmin() internal view returns (address) {
 132 |     |         return StorageSlotUpgradeable.getAddressSlot(_ADMIN_SLOT).value;
 133 |     |     }
 134 |     | 
 135 |     |     /**
 136 |     |      * @dev Stores a new address in the EIP1967 admin slot.
 137 |     |      */
 138 |     |     function _setAdmin(address newAdmin) private {
 139 |     |         require(newAdmin != address(0), "ERC1967: new admin is the zero address");
 140 |     |         StorageSlotUpgradeable.getAddressSlot(_ADMIN_SLOT).value = newAdmin;
 141 |     |     }
 142 |     | 
 143 |     |     /**
 144 |     |      * @dev Changes the admin of the proxy.
 145 |     |      *
 146 |     |      * Emits an {AdminChanged} event.
 147 |     |      */
 148 |     |     function _changeAdmin(address newAdmin) internal {
 149 |     |         emit AdminChanged(_getAdmin(), newAdmin);
 150 |     |         _setAdmin(newAdmin);
 151 |     |     }
 152 |     | 
 153 |     |     /**
 154 |     |      * @dev The storage slot of the UpgradeableBeacon contract which defines the implementation for this proxy.
 155 |     |      * This is bytes32(uint256(keccak256('eip1967.proxy.beacon')) - 1)) and is validated in the constructor.
 156 |     |      */
 157 |     |     bytes32 internal constant _BEACON_SLOT = 0xa3f0ad74e5423aebfd80d3ef4346578335a9a72aeaee59ff6cb3582b35133d50;
 158 |     | 
 159 |     |     /**
 160 |     |      * @dev Emitted when the beacon is upgraded.
 161 |     |      */
 162 |     |     event BeaconUpgraded(address indexed beacon);
 163 |     | 
 164 |     |     /**
 165 |     |      * @dev Returns the current beacon.
 166 |     |      */
 167 |     |     function _getBeacon() internal view returns (address) {
 168 |     |         return StorageSlotUpgradeable.getAddressSlot(_BEACON_SLOT).value;
 169 |     |     }
 170 |     | 
 171 |     |     /**
 172 |     |      * @dev Stores a new beacon in the EIP1967 beacon slot.
 173 |     |      */
 174 |     |     function _setBeacon(address newBeacon) private {
 175 |     |         require(AddressUpgradeable.isContract(newBeacon), "ERC1967: new beacon is not a contract");
 176 |     |         require(
 177 |     |             AddressUpgradeable.isContract(IBeaconUpgradeable(newBeacon).implementation()),
 178 |     |             "ERC1967: beacon implementation is not a contract"
 179 |     |         );
 180 |     |         StorageSlotUpgradeable.getAddressSlot(_BEACON_SLOT).value = newBeacon;
 181 |     |     }
 182 |     | 
 183 |     |     /**
 184 |     |      * @dev Perform beacon upgrade with additional setup call. Note: This upgrades the address of the beacon, it does
 185 |     |      * not upgrade the implementation contained in the beacon (see {UpgradeableBeacon-_setImplementation} for that).
 186 |     |      *
 187 |     |      * Emits a {BeaconUpgraded} event.
 188 |     |      */
 189 |     |     function _upgradeBeaconToAndCall(
 190 |     |         address newBeacon,
 191 |     |         bytes memory data,
 192 |     |         bool forceCall
 193 |     |     ) internal {
 194 |     |         _setBeacon(newBeacon);
 195 |     |         emit BeaconUpgraded(newBeacon);
 196 |     |         if (data.length > 0 || forceCall) {
 197 |     |             _functionDelegateCall(IBeaconUpgradeable(newBeacon).implementation(), data);
 198 |     |         }
 199 |     |     }
 200 |     | 
 201 |     |     /**
 202 |     |      * @dev Same as {xref-Address-functionCall-address-bytes-string-}[`functionCall`],
 203 |     |      * but performing a delegate call.
 204 |     |      *
 205 |     |      * _Available since v3.4._
 206 |     |      */
 207 |     |     function _functionDelegateCall(address target, bytes memory data) private returns (bytes memory) {
 208 |     |         require(AddressUpgradeable.isContract(target), "Address: delegate call to non-contract");
 209 |     | 
 210 |     |         // solhint-disable-next-line avoid-low-level-calls
 211 |     |         (bool success, bytes memory returndata) = target.delegatecall(data);
 212 |     |         return AddressUpgradeable.verifyCallResult(success, returndata, "Address: low-level delegate call failed");
 213 |     |     }
 214 |     |     uint256[50] private __gap;
 215 |     | }
