 240 |     |     /**
 241 |     |      * @dev Safely mints `tokenId` and transfers it to `to`.
 242 |     |      *
 243 |     |      * Requirements:
 244 |     |      *
 245 |     |      * - `tokenId` must not exist.
 246 |     |      * - If `to` refers to a smart contract, it must implement {IERC721Receiver-onERC721Received}, which is called upon a safe transfer.
 247 |     |      *
 248 |     |      * Emits a {Transfer} event.
 249 |     |      */
 250 |     |     function _safeMint(address to, uint256 tokenId) internal virtual {
 251 |     |         _safeMint(to, tokenId, "");
 252 |     |     }
 253 |     | 
 254 |     |     /**
 255 |     |      * @dev Same as {xref-ERC721-_safeMint-address-uint256-}[`_safeMint`], with an additional `data` parameter which is
 256 |     |      * forwarded in {IERC721Receiver-onERC721Received} to contract recipients.
 257 |     |      */
 258 |     |     function _safeMint(
 259 |     |         address to,
 260 |     |         uint256 tokenId,
 261 |     |         bytes memory _data
 262 |     |     ) internal virtual {
 263 |     |         _mint(to, tokenId);
 264 |     |         require(
 265 |     |             _checkOnERC721Received(address(0), to, tokenId, _data),
 266 |     |             "ERC721: transfer to non ERC721Receiver implementer"
 267 |     |         );
 268 |     |     }
 269 |     | 
 270 |     |     /**
 271 |     |      * @dev Mints `tokenId` and transfers it to `to`.
 272 |     |      *
 273 |     |      * WARNING: Usage of this method is discouraged, use {_safeMint} whenever possible
 274 |     |      *
 275 |     |      * Requirements:
 276 |     |      *
 277 |     |      * - `tokenId` must not exist.
 278 |     |      * - `to` cannot be the zero address.
 279 |     |      *
 280 |     |      * Emits a {Transfer} event.
 281 |     |      */
 282 |     |     function _mint(address to, uint256 tokenId) internal virtual {
 283 |     |         require(to != address(0), "ERC721: mint to the zero address");
 284 |     |         require(!_exists(tokenId), "ERC721: token already minted");
 285 |     | 
 286 |     |         _beforeTokenTransfer(address(0), to, tokenId);
 287 |     | 
 288 |     |         _balances[to] += 1;
 289 |     |         _owners[tokenId] = to;
 290 |     | 
 291 |     |         emit Transfer(address(0), to, tokenId);
 292 |     |     }
 293 |     | 
 294 |     |     /**
 295 |     |      * @dev Destroys `tokenId`.
 296 |     |      * The approval is cleared when the token is burned.
 297 |     |      *
 298 |     |      * Requirements:
 299 |     |      *
 300 |     |      * - `tokenId` must exist.
 301 |     |      *
 302 |     |      * Emits a {Transfer} event.
 303 |     |      */
 304 |     |     function _burn(uint256 tokenId) internal virtual {
 305 |     |         address owner = ERC721.ownerOf(tokenId);
 306 |     | 
 307 |     |         _beforeTokenTransfer(owner, address(0), tokenId);
 308 |     | 
 309 |     |         // Clear approvals
 310 |     |         _approve(address(0), tokenId);
 311 |     | 
 312 |     |         _balances[owner] -= 1;
 313 |     |         delete _owners[tokenId];
 314 |     | 
 315 |     |         emit Transfer(owner, address(0), tokenId);
 316 |     |     }
 317 |     | 
 318 |     |     /**
 319 |     |      * @dev Transfers `tokenId` from `from` to `to`.
 320 |     |      *  As opposed to {transferFrom}, this imposes no restrictions on msg.sender.
 321 |     |      *
 322 |     |      * Requirements:
 323 |     |      *
 324 |     |      * - `to` cannot be the zero address.
 325 |     |      * - `tokenId` token must be owned by `from`.
 326 |     |      *
 327 |     |      * Emits a {Transfer} event.
 328 |     |      */
 329 |     |     function _transfer(
 330 |     |         address from,
 331 |     |         address to,
 332 |     |         uint256 tokenId
 333 |     |     ) internal virtual {
 334 |     |         require(ERC721.ownerOf(tokenId) == from, "ERC721: transfer of token that is not own");
 335 |     |         require(to != address(0), "ERC721: transfer to the zero address");
 336 |     | 
 337 |     |         _beforeTokenTransfer(from, to, tokenId);
 338 |     | 
 339 |     |         // Clear approvals from the previous owner
 340 |     |         _approve(address(0), tokenId);
 341 |     | 
 342 |     |         _balances[from] -= 1;
 343 |     |         _balances[to] += 1;
 344 |     |         _owners[tokenId] = to;
 345 |     | 
 346 |     |         emit Transfer(from, to, tokenId);
 347 |     |     }
 348 |     | 
 349 |     |     /**
 350 |     |      * @dev Approve `to` to operate on `tokenId`
 351 |     |      *
 352 |     |      * Emits a {Approval} event.
 353 |     |      */
 354 |     |     function _approve(address to, uint256 tokenId) internal virtual {
 355 |     |         _tokenApprovals[tokenId] = to;
 356 |     |         emit Approval(ERC721.ownerOf(tokenId), to, tokenId);
 357 |     |     }
 358 |     | 
 359 |     |     /**
 360 |     |      * @dev Internal function to invoke {IERC721Receiver-onERC721Received} on a target address.
 361 |     |      * The call is not executed if the target address is not a contract.
 362 |     |      *
 363 |     |      * @param from address representing the previous owner of the given token ID
 364 |     |      * @param to target address that will receive the tokens
 365 |     |      * @param tokenId uint256 ID of the token to be transferred
 366 |     |      * @param _data bytes optional data to send along with the call
 367 |     |      * @return bool whether the call correctly returned the expected magic value
 368 |     |      */
 369 |     |     function _checkOnERC721Received(
 370 |     |         address from,
 371 |     |         address to,
 372 |     |         uint256 tokenId,
 373 |     |         bytes memory _data
 374 |     |     ) private returns (bool) {
 375 |     |         if (to.isContract()) {
 376 |     |             try IERC721Receiver(to).onERC721Received(_msgSender(), from, tokenId, _data) returns (bytes4 retval) {
 377 |     |                 return retval == IERC721Receiver.onERC721Received.selector;
 378 |     |             } catch (bytes memory reason) {
 379 |     |                 if (reason.length == 0) {
 380 |     |                     revert("ERC721: transfer to non ERC721Receiver implementer");
 381 |     |                 } else {
 382 |     |                     assembly {
 383 |     |                         revert(add(32, reason), mload(reason))
 384 |     |                     }
 385 |     |                 }
 386 |     |             }
 387 |     |         } else {
 388 |     |             return true;
 389 |     |         }
 390 |     |     }
 391 |     | 
 392 |     |     /**
 393 |     |      * @dev Hook that is called before any token transfer. This includes minting
 394 |     |      * and burning.
 395 |     |      *
 396 |     |      * Calling conditions:
 397 |     |      *
 398 |     |      * - When `from` and `to` are both non-zero, ``from``'s `tokenId` will be
 399 |     |      * transferred to `to`.
 400 |     |      * - When `from` is zero, `tokenId` will be minted for `to`.
 401 |     |      * - When `to` is zero, ``from``'s `tokenId` will be burned.
 402 |     |      * - `from` and `to` are never both zero.
 403 |     |      *
 404 |     |      * To learn more about hooks, head to xref:ROOT:extending-contracts.adoc#using-hooks[Using Hooks].
 405 |     |      */
 406 |     |     function _beforeTokenTransfer(
 407 |     |         address from,
 408 |     |         address to,
 409 |     |         uint256 tokenId
 410 |     |     ) internal virtual {}
 411 |     | }

   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2 |     | 
   3 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
   4 |     | 
   5 |     | import "../../utils/introspection/IERC165.sol";
   6 |     | 
   7 |     | /**
   8 |     |  * @dev Required interface of an ERC721 compliant contract.
   9 |     |  */
  10 |     | interface IERC721 is IERC165 {
  11 |     |     /**
  12 |     |      * @dev Emitted when `tokenId` token is transferred from `from` to `to`.
  13 |     |      */
  14 |     |     event Transfer(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 indexed tokenId);
  15 |     | 
  16 |     |     /**
  17 |     |      * @dev Emitted when `owner` enables `approved` to manage the `tokenId` token.
  18 |     |      */
  19 |     |     event Approval(address indexed owner, address indexed approved, uint256 indexed tokenId);
  20 |     | 
  21 |     |     /**
  22 |     |      * @dev Emitted when `owner` enables or disables (`approved`) `operator` to manage all of its assets.
  23 |     |      */
  24 |     |     event ApprovalForAll(address indexed owner, address indexed operator, bool approved);
  25 |     | 
  26 |     |     /**
  27 |     |      * @dev Returns the number of tokens in ``owner``'s account.
  28 |     |      */
  29 |     |     function balanceOf(address owner) external view returns (uint256 balance);
  30 |     | 
  31 |     |     /**
  32 |     |      * @dev Returns the owner of the `tokenId` token.
  33 |     |      *
  34 |     |      * Requirements:
  35 |     |      *
  36 |     |      * - `tokenId` must exist.
  37 |     |      */
  38 |     |     function ownerOf(uint256 tokenId) external view returns (address owner);
  39 |     | 
  40 |     |     /**
  41 |     |      * @dev Safely transfers `tokenId` token from `from` to `to`, checking first that contract recipients
  42 |     |      * are aware of the ERC721 protocol to prevent tokens from being forever locked.
  43 |     |      *
  44 |     |      * Requirements:
  45 |     |      *
  46 |     |      * - `from` cannot be the zero address.
  47 |     |      * - `to` cannot be the zero address.
  48 |     |      * - `tokenId` token must exist and be owned by `from`.
  49 |     |      * - If the caller is not `from`, it must be have been allowed to move this token by either {approve} or {setApprovalForAll}.
  50 |     |      * - If `to` refers to a smart contract, it must implement {IERC721Receiver-onERC721Received}, which is called upon a safe transfer.
  51 |     |      *
  52 |     |      * Emits a {Transfer} event.
  53 |     |      */
  54 |     |     function safeTransferFrom(
  55 |     |         address from,
  56 |     |         address to,
  57 |     |         uint256 tokenId
  58 |     |     ) external;
  59 |     | 
  60 |     |     /**
  61 |     |      * @dev Transfers `tokenId` token from `from` to `to`.
  62 |     |      *
  63 |     |      * WARNING: Usage of this method is discouraged, use {safeTransferFrom} whenever possible.
  64 |     |      *
  65 |     |      * Requirements:
  66 |     |      *
  67 |     |      * - `from` cannot be the zero address.
  68 |     |      * - `to` cannot be the zero address.
  69 |     |      * - `tokenId` token must be owned by `from`.
  70 |     |      * - If the caller is not `from`, it must be approved to move this token by either {approve} or {setApprovalForAll}.
  71 |     |      *
  72 |     |      * Emits a {Transfer} event.
  73 |     |      */
  74 |     |     function transferFrom(
  75 |     |         address from,
  76 |     |         address to,
  77 |     |         uint256 tokenId
  78 |     |     ) external;
  79 |     | 
  80 |     |     /**
  81 |     |      * @dev Gives permission to `to` to transfer `tokenId` token to another account.
  82 |     |      * The approval is cleared when the token is transferred.
  83 |     |      *
  84 |     |      * Only a single account can be approved at a time, so approving the zero address clears previous approvals.
  85 |     |      *
  86 |     |      * Requirements:
  87 |     |      *
  88 |     |      * - The caller must own the token or be an approved operator.
  89 |     |      * - `tokenId` must exist.
  90 |     |      *
  91 |     |      * Emits an {Approval} event.
  92 |     |      */
  93 |     |     function approve(address to, uint256 tokenId) external;
  94 |     | 
  95 |     |     /**
  96 |     |      * @dev Returns the account approved for `tokenId` token.
  97 |     |      *
  98 |     |      * Requirements:
  99 |     |      *
 100 |     |      * - `tokenId` must exist.
 101 |     |      */
 102 |     |     function getApproved(uint256 tokenId) external view returns (address operator);
 103 |     | 
 104 |     |     /**
 105 |     |      * @dev Approve or remove `operator` as an operator for the caller.
 106 |     |      * Operators can call {transferFrom} or {safeTransferFrom} for any token owned by the caller.
 107 |     |      *
 108 |     |      * Requirements:
 109 |     |      *
 110 |     |      * - The `operator` cannot be the caller.
 111 |     |      *
 112 |     |      * Emits an {ApprovalForAll} event.
 113 |     |      */
 114 |     |     function setApprovalForAll(address operator, bool _approved) external;
 115 |     | 
 116 |     |     /**
 117 |     |      * @dev Returns if the `operator` is allowed to manage all of the assets of `owner`.
 118 |     |      *
 119 |     |      * See {setApprovalForAll}
 120 |     |      */
 121 |     |     function isApprovedForAll(address owner, address operator) external view returns (bool);
 122 |     | 
 123 |     |     /**
 124 |     |      * @dev Safely transfers `tokenId` token from `from` to `to`.
 125 |     |      *
 126 |     |      * Requirements:
 127 |     |      *
 128 |     |      * - `from` cannot be the zero address.
 129 |     |      * - `to` cannot be the zero address.
 130 |     |      * - `tokenId` token must exist and be owned by `from`.
 131 |     |      * - If the caller is not `from`, it must be approved to move this token by either {approve} or {setApprovalForAll}.
 132 |     |      * - If `to` refers to a smart contract, it must implement {IERC721Receiver-onERC721Received}, which is called upon a safe transfer.
 133 |     |      *
 134 |     |      * Emits a {Transfer} event.
 135 |     |      */
 136 |     |     function safeTransferFrom(
 137 |     |         address from,

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Address.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2 |     | 
   3 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
   4 |     | 
   5 |     | /**
   6 |     |  * @dev Collection of functions related to the address type
   7 |     |  */
   8 |     | library Address {
   9 |     |     /**
  10 |     |      * @dev Returns true if `account` is a contract.
  11 |     |      *
  12 |     |      * [IMPORTANT]
  13 |     |      * ====
  14 |     |      * It is unsafe to assume that an address for which this function returns
  15 |     |      * false is an externally-owned account (EOA) and not a contract.
  16 |     |      *
  17 |     |      * Among others, `isContract` will return false for the following
  18 |     |      * types of addresses:
  19 |     |      *
  20 |     |      *  - an externally-owned account
  21 |     |      *  - a contract in construction
  22 |     |      *  - an address where a contract will be created
  23 |     |      *  - an address where a contract lived, but was destroyed
  24 |     |      * ====
  25 |     |      */
  26 |     |     function isContract(address account) internal view returns (bool) {
  27 |     |         // This method relies on extcodesize, which returns 0 for contracts in
  28 |     |         // construction, since the code is only stored at the end of the
  29 |     |         // constructor execution.
  30 |     | 
  31 |     |         uint256 size;
  32 |     |         assembly {
  33 |     |             size := extcodesize(account)
  34 |     |         }
  35 |     |         return size > 0;
  36 |     |     }
  37 |     | 
  38 |     |     /**
  39 |     |      * @dev Replacement for Solidity's `transfer`: sends `amount` wei to
  40 |     |      * `recipient`, forwarding all available gas and reverting on errors.
  41 |     |      *
  42 |     |      * https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-1884[EIP1884] increases the gas cost
  43 |     |      * of certain opcodes, possibly making contracts go over the 2300 gas limit
  44 |     |      * imposed by `transfer`, making them unable to receive funds via
  45 |     |      * `transfer`. {sendValue} removes this limitation.
  46 |     |      *
  47 |     |      * https://diligence.consensys.net/posts/2019/09/stop-using-soliditys-transfer-now/[Learn more].
  48 |     |      *
  49 |     |      * IMPORTANT: because control is transferred to `recipient`, care must be
  50 |     |      * taken to not create reentrancy vulnerabilities. Consider using
  51 |     |      * {ReentrancyGuard} or the
  52 |     |      * https://solidity.readthedocs.io/en/v0.5.11/security-considerations.html#use-the-checks-effects-interactions-pattern[checks-effects-interactions pattern].
  53 |     |      */
  54 |     |     function sendValue(address payable recipient, uint256 amount) internal {
  55 |     |         require(address(this).balance >= amount, "Address: insufficient balance");
  56 |     | 
  57 |     |         (bool success, ) = recipient.call{value: amount}("");
  58 |     |         require(success, "Address: unable to send value, recipient may have reverted");
  59 |     |     }
  60 |     | 
  61 |     |     /**
  62 |     |      * @dev Performs a Solidity function call using a low level `call`. A
  63 |     |      * plain `call` is an unsafe replacement for a function call: use this
  64 |     |      * function instead.
  65 |     |      *
  66 |     |      * If `target` reverts with a revert reason, it is bubbled up by this
  67 |     |      * function (like regular Solidity function calls).
  68 |     |      *
  69 |     |      * Returns the raw returned data. To convert to the expected return value,
  70 |     |      * use https://solidity.readthedocs.io/en/latest/units-and-global-variables.html?highlight=abi.decode#abi-encoding-and-decoding-functions[`abi.decode`].
  71 |     |      *
  72 |     |      * Requirements:
  73 |     |      *
  74 |     |      * - `target` must be a contract.
  75 |     |      * - calling `target` with `data` must not revert.
  76 |     |      *
  77 |     |      * _Available since v3.1._
  78 |     |      */
  79 |     |     function functionCall(address target, bytes memory data) internal returns (bytes memory) {
  80 |     |         return functionCall(target, data, "Address: low-level call failed");
  81 |     |     }
  82 |     | 
  83 |     |     /**
  84 |     |      * @dev Same as {xref-Address-functionCall-address-bytes-}[`functionCall`], but with
  85 |     |      * `errorMessage` as a fallback revert reason when `target` reverts.
  86 |     |      *
  87 |     |      * _Available since v3.1._
  88 |     |      */
  89 |     |     function functionCall(
  90 |     |         address target,
  91 |     |         bytes memory data,
  92 |     |         string memory errorMessage
  93 |     |     ) internal returns (bytes memory) {
  94 |     |         return functionCallWithValue(target, data, 0, errorMessage);
  95 |     |     }
  96 |     | 
  97 |     |     /**
  98 |     |      * @dev Same as {xref-Address-functionCall-address-bytes-}[`functionCall`],
  99 |     |      * but also transferring `value` wei to `target`.
 100 |     |      *
 101 |     |      * Requirements:
 102 |     |      *
 103 |     |      * - the calling contract must have an ETH balance of at least `value`.
 104 |     |      * - the called Solidity function must be `payable`.
 105 |     |      *
 106 |     |      * _Available since v3.1._
 107 |     |      */
 108 |     |     function functionCallWithValue(
 109 |     |         address target,
 110 |     |         bytes memory data,
 111 |     |         uint256 value
 112 |     |     ) internal returns (bytes memory) {
 113 |     |         return functionCallWithValue(target, data, value, "Address: low-level call with value failed");
 114 |     |     }
 115 |     | 
 116 |     |     /**
 117 |     |      * @dev Same as {xref-Address-functionCallWithValue-address-bytes-uint256-}[`functionCallWithValue`], but
 118 |     |      * with `errorMessage` as a fallback revert reason when `target` reverts.
 119 |     |      *
 120 |     |      * _Available since v3.1._
 121 |     |      */
 122 |     |     function functionCallWithValue(
 123 |     |         address target,
 124 |     |         bytes memory data,
 125 |     |         uint256 value,
 126 |     |         string memory errorMessage
 127 |     |     ) internal returns (bytes memory) {
 128 |     |         require(address(this).balance >= value, "Address: insufficient balance for call");
 129 |     |         require(isContract(target), "Address: call to non-contract");
 130 |     | 
 131 |     |         (bool success, bytes memory returndata) = target.call{value: value}(data);
 132 |     |         return verifyCallResult(success, returndata, errorMessage);
 133 |     |     }
 134 |     | 
 135 |     |     /**
 136 |     |      * @dev Same as {xref-Address-functionCall-address-bytes-}[`functionCall`],
 137 |     |      * but performing a static call.
 138 |     |      *
 139 |     |      * _Available since v3.3._
 140 |     |      */
 141 |     |     function functionStaticCall(address target, bytes memory data) internal view returns (bytes memory) {
 142 |     |         return functionStaticCall(target, data, "Address: low-level static call failed");
 143 |     |     }
 144 |     | 
 145 |     |     /**
 146 |     |      * @dev Same as {xref-Address-functionCall-address-bytes-string-}[`functionCall`],
 147 |     |      * but performing a static call.
 148 |     |      *
 149 |     |      * _Available since v3.3._
 150 |     |      */
 151 |     |     function functionStaticCall(
 152 |     |         address target,
 153 |     |         bytes memory data,
 154 |     |         string memory errorMessage
 155 |     |     ) internal view returns (bytes memory) {
 156 |     |         require(isContract(target), "Address: static call to non-contract");
 157 |     | 
 158 |     |         (bool success, bytes memory returndata) = target.staticcall(data);
 159 |     |         return verifyCallResult(success, returndata, errorMessage);
 160 |     |     }
 161 |     | 
 162 |     |     /**
 163 |     |      * @dev Same as {xref-Address-functionCall-address-bytes-}[`functionCall`],
 164 |     |      * but performing a delegate call.
 165 |     |      *
 166 |     |      * _Available since v3.4._
 167 |     |      */
 168 |     |     function functionDelegateCall(address target, bytes memory data) internal returns (bytes memory) {
 169 |     |         return functionDelegateCall(target, data, "Address: low-level delegate call failed");
 170 |     |     }
 171 |     | 
 172 |     |     /**
 173 |     |      * @dev Same as {xref-Address-functionCall-address-bytes-string-}[`functionCall`],
 174 |     |      * but performing a delegate call.
 175 |     |      *
 176 |     |      * _Available since v3.4._
 177 |     |      */
 178 |     |     function functionDelegateCall(
 179 |     |         address target,
 180 |     |         bytes memory data,
 181 |     |         string memory errorMessage
 182 |     |     ) internal returns (bytes memory) {
 183 |     |         require(isContract(target), "Address: delegate call to non-contract");
 184 |     | 
 185 |     |         (bool success, bytes memory returndata) = target.delegatecall(data);
 186 |     |         return verifyCallResult(success, returndata, errorMessage);
 187 |     |     }
 188 |     | 
 189 |     |     /**
 190 |     |      * @dev Tool to verifies that a low level call was successful, and revert if it wasn't, either by bubbling the
 191 |     |      * revert reason using the provided one.
 192 |     |      *
 193 |     |      * _Available since v4.3._
 194 |     |      */
 195 |     |     function verifyCallResult(
 196 |     |         bool success,
 197 |     |         bytes memory returndata,
 198 |     |         string memory errorMessage
 199 |     |     ) internal pure returns (bytes memory) {
 200 |     |         if (success) {
 201 |     |             return returndata;
 202 |     |         } else {
 203 |     |             // Look for revert reason and bubble it up if present
 204 |     |             if (returndata.length > 0) {
 205 |     |                 // The easiest way to bubble the revert reason is using memory via assembly
 206 |     | 
 207 |     |                 assembly {
 208 |     |                     let returndata_size := mload(returndata)
 209 |     |                     revert(add(32, returndata), returndata_size)
 210 |     |                 }
 211 |     |             } else {
 212 |     |                 revert(errorMessage);
 213 |     |             }
 214 |     |         }
 215 |     |     }
 216 |     | }

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Arrays.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
  2 |     | 
  3 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
  4 |     | 
  5 |     | import "./math/Math.sol";
  6 |     | 
  7 |     | /**
  8 |     |  * @dev Collection of functions related to array types.
  9 |     |  */
 10 |     | library Arrays {
 11 |     |     /**
 12 |     |      * @dev Searches a sorted `array` and returns the first index that contains
 13 |     |      * a value greater or equal to `element`. If no such index exists (i.e. all
 14 |     |      * values in the array are strictly less than `element`), the array length is
 15 |     |      * returned. Time complexity O(log n).
 16 |     |      *
 17 |     |      * `array` is expected to be sorted in ascending order, and to contain no
 18 |     |      * repeated elements.
 19 |     |      */
 20 |     |     function findUpperBound(uint256[] storage array, uint256 element) internal view returns (uint256) {
 21 |     |         if (array.length == 0) {
 22 |     |             return 0;
 23 |     |         }
 24 |     | 
 25 |     |         uint256 low = 0;
 26 |     |         uint256 high = array.length;
 27 |     | 
 28 |     |         while (low < high) {
 29 |     |             uint256 mid = Math.average(low, high);
 30 |     | 
 31 |     |             // Note that mid will always be strictly less than high (i.e. it will be a valid array index)
 32 |     |             // because Math.average rounds down (it does integer division with truncation).
 33 |     |             if (array[mid] > element) {
 34 |     |                 high = mid;
 35 |     |             } else {
 36 |     |                 low = mid + 1;
 37 |     |             }
 38 |     |         }
 39 |     | 
 40 |     |         // At this point `low` is the exclusive upper bound. We will return the inclusive upper bound.
 41 |     |         if (low > 0 && array[low - 1] == element) {
 42 |     |             return low - 1;
 43 |     |         } else {
 44 |     |             return low;
 45 |     |         }
 46 |     |     }
 47 |     | }
 48 |     |

  3 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
  4 |     | 
  5 |     | /**
  6 |     |  * @dev Provides information about the current execution context, including the
  7 |     |  * sender of the transaction and its data. While these are generally available
  8 |     |  * via msg.sender and msg.data, they should not be accessed in such a direct
  9 |     |  * manner, since when dealing with meta-transactions the account sending and
 10 |     |  * paying for execution may not be the actual sender (as far as an application
 11 |     |  * is concerned).
 12 |     |  *
 13 |     |  * This contract is only required for intermediate, library-like contracts.
 14 |     |  */
 15 |     | abstract contract Context {
 16 |     |     function _msgSender() internal view virtual returns (address) {
 17 |     |         return msg.sender;
 18 |     |     }
 19 |     | 
 20 |     |     function _msgData() internal view virtual returns (bytes calldata) {
 21 |     |         return msg.data;
 22 |     |     }

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Counters.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
  2 |     | 
  3 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
  4 |     | 
  5 |     | /**
  6 |     |  * @title Counters
  7 |     |  * @author Matt Condon (@shrugs)
  8 |     |  * @dev Provides counters that can only be incremented, decremented or reset. This can be used e.g. to track the number
  9 |     |  * of elements in a mapping, issuing ERC721 ids, or counting request ids.
 10 |     |  *
 11 |     |  * Include with `using Counters for Counters.Counter;`
 12 |     |  */
 13 |     | library Counters {
 14 |     |     struct Counter {
 15 |     |         // This variable should never be directly accessed by users of the library: interactions must be restricted to
 16 |     |         // the library's function. As of Solidity v0.5.2, this cannot be enforced, though there is a proposal to add
 17 |     |         // this feature: see https://github.com/ethereum/solidity/issues/4637
 18 |     |         uint256 _value; // default: 0
 19 |     |     }
 20 |     | 
 21 |     |     function current(Counter storage counter) internal view returns (uint256) {
 22 |     |         return counter._value;
 23 |     |     }
 24 |     | 
 25 |     |     function increment(Counter storage counter) internal {
 26 |     |         unchecked {
 27 |     |             counter._value += 1;
 28 |     |         }
 29 |     |     }
 30 |     | 
 31 |     |     function decrement(Counter storage counter) internal {
 32 |     |         uint256 value = counter._value;
 33 |     |         require(value > 0, "Counter: decrement overflow");
 34 |     |         unchecked {
 35 |     |             counter._value = value - 1;
 36 |     |         }
 37 |     |     }
 38 |     | 
 39 |     |     function reset(Counter storage counter) internal {
 40 |     |         counter._value = 0;
 41 |     |     }
 42 |     | }
 43 |     | 

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Strings.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
  2 |     | 
  3 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
  4 |     | 
  5 |     | /**
  6 |     |  * @dev String operations.
  7 |     |  */
  8 |     | library Strings {
  9 |     |     bytes16 private constant _HEX_SYMBOLS = "0123456789abcdef";
 10 |     | 
 11 |     |     /**
 12 |     |      * @dev Converts a `uint256` to its ASCII `string` decimal representation.
 13 |     |      */
 14 |     |     function toString(uint256 value) internal pure returns (string memory) {
 15 |     |         // Inspired by OraclizeAPI's implementation - MIT licence
 16 |     |         // https://github.com/oraclize/ethereum-api/blob/b42146b063c7d6ee1358846c198246239e9360e8/oraclizeAPI_0.4.25.sol
 17 |     | 
 18 |     |         if (value == 0) {
 19 |     |             return "0";
 20 |     |         }
 21 |     |         uint256 temp = value;
 22 |     |         uint256 digits;
 23 |     |         while (temp != 0) {
 24 |     |             digits++;
 25 |     |             temp /= 10;
 26 |     |         }
 27 |     |         bytes memory buffer = new bytes(digits);
 28 |     |         while (value != 0) {
 29 |     |             digits -= 1;
 30 |     |             buffer[digits] = bytes1(uint8(48 + uint256(value % 10)));
 31 |     |             value /= 10;
 32 |     |         }
 33 |     |         return string(buffer);
 34 |     |     }
 35 |     | 
 36 |     |     /**
 37 |     |      * @dev Converts a `uint256` to its ASCII `string` hexadecimal representation.
 38 |     |      */
 39 |     |     function toHexString(uint256 value) internal pure returns (string memory) {
 40 |     |         if (value == 0) {
 41 |     |             return "0x00";
 42 |     |         }
 43 |     |         uint256 temp = value;
 44 |     |         uint256 length = 0;
 45 |     |         while (temp != 0) {
 46 |     |             length++;
 47 |     |             temp >>= 8;
 48 |     |         }
 49 |     |         return toHexString(value, length);
 50 |     |     }
 51 |     | 
 52 |     |     /**
 53 |     |      * @dev Converts a `uint256` to its ASCII `string` hexadecimal representation with fixed length.
 54 |     |      */
 55 |     |     function toHexString(uint256 value, uint256 length) internal pure returns (string memory) {
 56 |     |         bytes memory buffer = new bytes(2 * length + 2);
 57 |     |         buffer[0] = "0";
 58 |     |         buffer[1] = "x";
 59 |     |         for (uint256 i = 2 * length + 1; i > 1; --i) {
 60 |     |             buffer[i] = _HEX_SYMBOLS[value & 0xf];
 61 |     |             value >>= 4;
 62 |     |         }
 63 |     |         require(value == 0, "Strings: hex length insufficient");
 64 |     |         return string(buffer);
 65 |     |     }
 66 |     | }

  6 |     | 
  7 |     | /**
  8 |     |  * @dev Implementation of the {IERC165} interface.
  9 |     |  *
 10 |     |  * Contracts that want to implement ERC165 should inherit from this contract and override {supportsInterface} to check
 11 |     |  * for the additional interface id that will be supported. For example:
 12 |     |  *
 13 |     |  * ```solidity
 14 |     |  * function supportsInterface(bytes4 interfaceId) public view virtual override returns (bool) {
 15 |     |  *     return interfaceId == type(MyInterface).interfaceId || super.supportsInterface(interfaceId);
 16 |     |  * }
 17 |     |  * ```
 18 |     |  *
 19 |     |  * Alternatively, {ERC165Storage} provides an easier to use but more expensive implementation.
 20 |     |  */
 21 |     | abstract contract ERC165 is IERC165 {
 22 |     |     /**
 23 |     |      * @dev See {IERC165-supportsInterface}.
 24 |     |      */
 25 |     |     function supportsInterface(bytes4 interfaceId) public view virtual override returns (bool) {
 26 |     |         return interfaceId == type(IERC165).interfaceId;
 27 |     |     }
 28 |     | }

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
  2 |     | 
  3 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
  4 |     | 
  5 |     | /**
  6 |     |  * @dev Standard math utilities missing in the Solidity language.
  7 |     |  */
  8 |     | library Math {
  9 |     |     /**
 10 |     |      * @dev Returns the largest of two numbers.
 11 |     |      */
 12 |     |     function max(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {
 13 |     |         return a >= b ? a : b;
 14 |     |     }
 15 |     | 
 16 |     |     /**
 17 |     |      * @dev Returns the smallest of two numbers.
 18 |     |      */
 19 |     |     function min(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {
 20 |     |         return a < b ? a : b;
 21 |     |     }
 22 |     | 
 23 |     |     /**
 24 |     |      * @dev Returns the average of two numbers. The result is rounded towards
 25 |     |      * zero.
 26 |     |      */
 27 |     |     function average(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {
 28 |     |         // (a + b) / 2 can overflow.
 29 |     |         return (a & b) + (a ^ b) / 2;
 30 |     |     }
 31 |     | 
 32 |     |     /**
 33 |     |      * @dev Returns the ceiling of the division of two numbers.
 34 |     |      *
 35 |     |      * This differs from standard division with `/` in that it rounds up instead
 36 |     |      * of rounding down.
 37 |     |      */
 38 |     |     function ceilDiv(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {
 39 |     |         // (a + b - 1) / b can overflow on addition, so we distribute.
 40 |     |         return a / b + (a % b == 0 ? 0 : 1);
 41 |     |     }
 42 |     | }
 43 |     | 

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/structs/EnumerableSet.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2 |     | 
   3 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
   4 |     | 
   5 |     | /**
   6 |     |  * @dev Library for managing
   7 |     |  * https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Set_(abstract_data_type)[sets] of primitive
   8 |     |  * types.
   9 |     |  *
  10 |     |  * Sets have the following properties:
  11 |     |  *
  12 |     |  * - Elements are added, removed, and checked for existence in constant time
  13 |     |  * (O(1)).
  14 |     |  * - Elements are enumerated in O(n). No guarantees are made on the ordering.
  15 |     |  *
  16 |     |  * ```
  17 |     |  * contract Example {
  18 |     |  *     // Add the library methods
  19 |     |  *     using EnumerableSet for EnumerableSet.AddressSet;
  20 |     |  *
  21 |     |  *     // Declare a set state variable
  22 |     |  *     EnumerableSet.AddressSet private mySet;
  23 |     |  * }
  24 |     |  * ```
  25 |     |  *
  26 |     |  * As of v3.3.0, sets of type `bytes32` (`Bytes32Set`), `address` (`AddressSet`)
  27 |     |  * and `uint256` (`UintSet`) are supported.
  28 |     |  */
  29 |     | library EnumerableSet {
  30 |     |     // To implement this library for multiple types with as little code
  31 |     |     // repetition as possible, we write it in terms of a generic Set type with
  32 |     |     // bytes32 values.
  33 |     |     // The Set implementation uses private functions, and user-facing
  34 |     |     // implementations (such as AddressSet) are just wrappers around the
  35 |     |     // underlying Set.
  36 |     |     // This means that we can only create new EnumerableSets for types that fit
  37 |     |     // in bytes32.
  38 |     | 
  39 |     |     struct Set {
  40 |     |         // Storage of set values
  41 |     |         bytes32[] _values;
  42 |     |         // Position of the value in the `values` array, plus 1 because index 0
  43 |     |         // means a value is not in the set.
  44 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => uint256) _indexes;
  45 |     |     }
  46 |     | 
  47 |     |     /**
  48 |     |      * @dev Add a value to a set. O(1).
  49 |     |      *
  50 |     |      * Returns true if the value was added to the set, that is if it was not
  51 |     |      * already present.
  52 |     |      */
  53 |     |     function _add(Set storage set, bytes32 value) private returns (bool) {
  54 |     |         if (!_contains(set, value)) {
  55 |     |             set._values.push(value);
  56 |     |             // The value is stored at length-1, but we add 1 to all indexes
  57 |     |             // and use 0 as a sentinel value
  58 |     |             set._indexes[value] = set._values.length;
  59 |     |             return true;
  60 |     |         } else {
  61 |     |             return false;
  62 |     |         }
  63 |     |     }
  64 |     | 
  65 |     |     /**
  66 |     |      * @dev Removes a value from a set. O(1).
  67 |     |      *
  68 |     |      * Returns true if the value was removed from the set, that is if it was
  69 |     |      * present.
  70 |     |      */
  71 |     |     function _remove(Set storage set, bytes32 value) private returns (bool) {
  72 |     |         // We read and store the value's index to prevent multiple reads from the same storage slot
  73 |     |         uint256 valueIndex = set._indexes[value];
  74 |     | 
  75 |     |         if (valueIndex != 0) {
  76 |     |             // Equivalent to contains(set, value)
  77 |     |             // To delete an element from the _values array in O(1), we swap the element to delete with the last one in
  78 |     |             // the array, and then remove the last element (sometimes called as 'swap and pop').
  79 |     |             // This modifies the order of the array, as noted in {at}.
  80 |     | 
  81 |     |             uint256 toDeleteIndex = valueIndex - 1;
  82 |     |             uint256 lastIndex = set._values.length - 1;
  83 |     | 
  84 |     |             if (lastIndex != toDeleteIndex) {
  85 |     |                 bytes32 lastvalue = set._values[lastIndex];
  86 |     | 
  87 |     |                 // Move the last value to the index where the value to delete is
  88 |     |                 set._values[toDeleteIndex] = lastvalue;
  89 |     |                 // Update the index for the moved value
  90 |     |                 set._indexes[lastvalue] = valueIndex; // Replace lastvalue's index to valueIndex
  91 |     |             }
  92 |     | 
  93 |     |             // Delete the slot where the moved value was stored
  94 |     |             set._values.pop();
  95 |     | 
  96 |     |             // Delete the index for the deleted slot
  97 |     |             delete set._indexes[value];
  98 |     | 
  99 |     |             return true;
 100 |     |         } else {
 101 |     |             return false;
 102 |     |         }
 103 |     |     }
 104 |     | 
 105 |     |     /**
 106 |     |      * @dev Returns true if the value is in the set. O(1).
 107 |     |      */
 108 |     |     function _contains(Set storage set, bytes32 value) private view returns (bool) {
 109 |     |         return set._indexes[value] != 0;
 110 |     |     }
 111 |     | 
 112 |     |     /**
 113 |     |      * @dev Returns the number of values on the set. O(1).
 114 |     |      */
 115 |     |     function _length(Set storage set) private view returns (uint256) {
 116 |     |         return set._values.length;
 117 |     |     }
 118 |     | 
 119 |     |     /**
 120 |     |      * @dev Returns the value stored at position `index` in the set. O(1).
 121 |     |      *
 122 |     |      * Note that there are no guarantees on the ordering of values inside the
 123 |     |      * array, and it may change when more values are added or removed.
 124 |     |      *
 125 |     |      * Requirements:
 126 |     |      *
 127 |     |      * - `index` must be strictly less than {length}.
 128 |     |      */
 129 |     |     function _at(Set storage set, uint256 index) private view returns (bytes32) {
 130 |     |         return set._values[index];
 131 |     |     }
 132 |     | 
 133 |     |     /**
 134 |     |      * @dev Return the entire set in an array
 135 |     |      *
 136 |     |      * WARNING: This operation will copy the entire storage to memory, which can be quite expensive. This is designed
 137 |     |      * to mostly be used by view accessors that are queried without any gas fees. Developers should keep in mind that
 138 |     |      * this function has an unbounded cost, and using it as part of a state-changing function may render the function
 139 |     |      * uncallable if the set grows to a point where copying to memory consumes too much gas to fit in a block.
 140 |     |      */
 141 |     |     function _values(Set storage set) private view returns (bytes32[] memory) {
 142 |     |         return set._values;
 143 |     |     }
 144 |     |

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/contracts/DamnValuableNFT.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
  2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC721/ERC721.sol";
  5 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC721/extensions/ERC721Burnable.sol";
  6 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/access/AccessControl.sol";

  8 |     |

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/contracts/backdoor/WalletRegistry.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
  2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol";
  5 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
  6 |     | import "@gnosis.pm/safe-contracts/contracts/GnosisSafe.sol";
  7 |     | import "@gnosis.pm/safe-contracts/contracts/proxies/IProxyCreationCallback.sol";
  8 |     | 
  9 |     | /**
 10 |     |  * @title WalletRegistry
 11 |     |  * @notice A registry for Gnosis Safe wallets.
 12 |     |            When known beneficiaries deploy and register their wallets, the registry sends some Damn Valuable Tokens to the wallet.
 13 |     |  * @dev The registry has embedded verifications to ensure only legitimate Gnosis Safe wallets are stored.
 14 |     |  * @author Damn Vulnerable DeFi (https://damnvulnerabledefi.xyz)
 15 |     |  */
 16 |     | contract WalletRegistry is IProxyCreationCallback, Ownable {
 17 |     |     
 18 |     |     uint256 private constant MAX_OWNERS = 1;
 19 |     |     uint256 private constant MAX_THRESHOLD = 1;
 20 |     |     uint256 private constant TOKEN_PAYMENT = 10 ether; // 10 * 10 ** 18
 21 |     |     
 22 |     |     address public immutable masterCopy;
 23 |     |     address public immutable walletFactory;
 24 |     |     IERC20 public immutable token;
 25 |     | 
 26 |     |     mapping (address => bool) public beneficiaries;
 27 |     | 
 28 |     |     // owner => wallet
 29 |     |     mapping (address => address) public wallets;
 30 |     | 
 31 |     |     constructor(
 32 |     |         address masterCopyAddress,
 33 |     |         address walletFactoryAddress, 
 34 |     |         address tokenAddress,
 35 |     |         address[] memory initialBeneficiaries
 36 |     |     ) {
 37 |     |         require(masterCopyAddress != address(0));
 38 |     |         require(walletFactoryAddress != address(0));
 39 |     | 
 40 |     |         masterCopy = masterCopyAddress;
 41 |     |         walletFactory = walletFactoryAddress;
 42 |     |         token = IERC20(tokenAddress);
 43 |     | 
 44 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < initialBeneficiaries.length; i++) {
 45 |     |             addBeneficiary(initialBeneficiaries[i]);
 46 |     |         }
 47 |     |     }
 48 |     | 
 49 |     |     function addBeneficiary(address beneficiary) public onlyOwner {
 50 |     |         beneficiaries[beneficiary] = true;
 51 |     |     }
 52 |     | 
 53 |     |     function _removeBeneficiary(address beneficiary) private {
 54 |     |         beneficiaries[beneficiary] = false;
 55 |     |     }
 56 |     | 
 57 |     |     /**
 58 |     |      @notice Function executed when user creates a Gnosis Safe wallet via GnosisSafeProxyFactory::createProxyWithCallback
 59 |     |              setting the registry's address as the callback.
 60 |     |      */
 61 |     |     function proxyCreated(
 62 |     |         GnosisSafeProxy proxy,
 63 |     |         address singleton,
 64 |     |         bytes calldata initializer,
 65 |     |         uint256
 66 |     |     ) external override {
 67 |     |         // Make sure we have enough DVT to pay
 68 |     |         require(token.balanceOf(address(this)) >= TOKEN_PAYMENT, "Not enough funds to pay");
 69 |     | 
 70 |     |         address payable walletAddress = payable(proxy);
 71 |     | 
 72 |     |         // Ensure correct factory and master copy
 73 |     |         require(msg.sender == walletFactory, "Caller must be factory");
 74 |     |         require(singleton == masterCopy, "Fake mastercopy used");
 75 |     |         
 76 |     |         // Ensure initial calldata was a call to `GnosisSafe::setup`
 77 |     |         require(bytes4(initializer[:4]) == GnosisSafe.setup.selector, "Wrong initialization");
 78 |     | 
 79 |     |         // Ensure wallet initialization is the expected
 80 |     |         require(GnosisSafe(walletAddress).getThreshold() == MAX_THRESHOLD, "Invalid threshold");
 81 |     |         require(GnosisSafe(walletAddress).getOwners().length == MAX_OWNERS, "Invalid number of owners");       
 82 |     | 
 83 |     |         // Ensure the owner is a registered beneficiary
 84 |     |         address walletOwner = GnosisSafe(walletAddress).getOwners()[0];
 85 |     | 
 86 |     |         require(beneficiaries[walletOwner], "Owner is not registered as beneficiary");
 87 |     | 
 88 |     |         // Remove owner as beneficiary
 89 |     |         _removeBeneficiary(walletOwner);
 90 |     | 
 91 |     |         // Register the wallet under the owner's address
 92 |     |         wallets[walletOwner] = walletAddress;
 93 |     | 
 94 |     |         // Pay tokens to the newly created wallet
 95 |     |         token.transfer(walletAddress, TOKEN_PAYMENT);        
 96 |     |     }
 97 |     | }

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/contracts/climber/ClimberTimelock.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
   3 |     | 
   4 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/access/AccessControl.sol";
   5 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Address.sol";
   6 |     | 
   7 |     | /**
   8 |     |  * @title ClimberTimelock
   9 |     |  * @author Damn Vulnerable DeFi (https://damnvulnerabledefi.xyz)
  10 |     |  */
  11 |     | contract ClimberTimelock is AccessControl {
  12 |     |     using Address for address;
  13 |     | 
  14 |     |     bytes32 public constant ADMIN_ROLE = keccak256("ADMIN_ROLE");
  15 |     |     bytes32 public constant PROPOSER_ROLE = keccak256("PROPOSER_ROLE");
  16 |     | 
  17 |     |     // Possible states for an operation in this timelock contract
  18 |     |     enum OperationState {
  19 |     |         Unknown,
  20 |     |         Scheduled,
  21 |     |         ReadyForExecution,
  22 |     |         Executed
  23 |     |     }
  24 |     | 
  25 |     |     // Operation data tracked in this contract
  26 |     |     struct Operation {
  27 |     |         uint64 readyAtTimestamp;   // timestamp at which the operation will be ready for execution
  28 |     |         bool known;         // whether the operation is registered in the timelock
  29 |     |         bool executed;      // whether the operation has been executed
  30 |     |     }
  31 |     | 
  32 |     |     // Operations are tracked by their bytes32 identifier
  33 |     |     mapping(bytes32 => Operation) public operations;
  34 |     | 
  35 |     |     uint64 public delay = 1 hours;
  36 |     | 
  37 |     |     constructor(
  38 |     |         address admin,
  39 |     |         address proposer
  40 |     |     ) {
  41 |     |         _setRoleAdmin(ADMIN_ROLE, ADMIN_ROLE);
  42 |     |         _setRoleAdmin(PROPOSER_ROLE, ADMIN_ROLE);
  43 |     | 
  44 |     |         // deployer + self administration
  45 |     |         _setupRole(ADMIN_ROLE, admin);
  46 |     |         _setupRole(ADMIN_ROLE, address(this));
  47 |     | 
  48 |     |         _setupRole(PROPOSER_ROLE, proposer);
  49 |     |     }
  50 |     | 
  51 |     |     function getOperationState(bytes32 id) public view returns (OperationState) {
  52 |     |         Operation memory op = operations[id];
  53 |     |         
  54 |     |         if(op.executed) {
  55 |     |             return OperationState.Executed;
  56 |     |         } else if(op.readyAtTimestamp >= block.timestamp) {
  57 |     |             return OperationState.ReadyForExecution;
  58 |     |         } else if(op.readyAtTimestamp > 0) {
  59 |     |             return OperationState.Scheduled;
  60 |     |         } else {
  61 |     |             return OperationState.Unknown;
  62 |     |         }
  63 |     |     }
  64 |     | 
  65 |     |     function getOperationId(
  66 |     |         address[] calldata targets,
  67 |     |         uint256[] calldata values,
  68 |     |         bytes[] calldata dataElements,
  69 |     |         bytes32 salt
  70 |     |     ) public pure returns (bytes32) {
  71 |     |         return keccak256(abi.encode(targets, values, dataElements, salt));
  72 |     |     }
  73 |     | 
  74 |     |     function schedule(
  75 |     |         address[] calldata targets,
  76 |     |         uint256[] calldata values,
  77 |     |         bytes[] calldata dataElements,
  78 |     |         bytes32 salt
  79 |     |     ) external onlyRole(PROPOSER_ROLE) {
  80 |     |         require(targets.length > 0 && targets.length < 256);
  81 |     |         require(targets.length == values.length);
  82 |     |         require(targets.length == dataElements.length);
  83 |     | 
  84 |     |         bytes32 id = getOperationId(targets, values, dataElements, salt);
  85 |     |         require(getOperationState(id) == OperationState.Unknown, "Operation already known");
  86 |     |         
  87 |     |         operations[id].readyAtTimestamp = uint64(block.timestamp) + delay;
  88 |     |         operations[id].known = true;
  89 |     |     }
  90 |     | 
  91 |     |     /** Anyone can execute what has been scheduled via `schedule` */
  92 |     |     function execute(
  93 |     |         address[] calldata targets,
  94 |     |         uint256[] calldata values,
  95 |     |         bytes[] calldata dataElements,
  96 |     |         bytes32 salt
  97 |     |     ) external payable {
  98 |     |         require(targets.length > 0, "Must provide at least one target");
  99 |     |         require(targets.length == values.length);
 100 |     |         require(targets.length == dataElements.length);
 101 |     | 
 102 |     |         bytes32 id = getOperationId(targets, values, dataElements, salt);
 103 |     | 
 104 |     |         for (uint8 i = 0; i < targets.length; i++) {
 105 |     |             targets[i].functionCallWithValue(dataElements[i], values[i]);
 106 |     |         }
 107 |     |         
 108 |     |         require(getOperationState(id) == OperationState.ReadyForExecution);
 109 |     |         operations[id].executed = true;
 110 |     |     }
 111 |     | 
 112 |     |     function updateDelay(uint64 newDelay) external {
 113 |     |         require(msg.sender == address(this), "Caller must be timelock itself");
 114 |     |         require(newDelay <= 14 days, "Delay must be 14 days or less");
 115 |     |         delay = newDelay;
 116 |     |     }
 117 |     | 
 118 |     |     receive() external payable {}
 119 |     | }
 120 |     |

  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
  2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/proxy/utils/Initializable.sol";
  5 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/access/OwnableUpgradeable.sol";
  6 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/proxy/utils/UUPSUpgradeable.sol";
  7 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
  8 |     | 
  9 |     | import "./ClimberTimelock.sol";
 10 |     | 
 11 |     | /**
 12 |     |  * @title ClimberVault
 13 |     |  * @dev To be deployed behind a proxy following the UUPS pattern. Upgrades are to be triggered by the owner.
 14 |     |  * @author Damn Vulnerable DeFi (https://damnvulnerabledefi.xyz)
 15 |     |  */
 16 |     | contract ClimberVault is Initializable, OwnableUpgradeable, UUPSUpgradeable {
 17 |     | 
 18 |     |     uint256 public constant WITHDRAWAL_LIMIT = 1 ether;
 19 |     |     uint256 public constant WAITING_PERIOD = 15 days;
 20 |     | 
 21 |     |     uint256 private _lastWithdrawalTimestamp;
 22 |     |     address private _sweeper;
 23 |     | 
 24 |     |     modifier onlySweeper() {
 25 |     |         require(msg.sender == _sweeper, "Caller must be sweeper");
 26 |     |         _;
 27 |     |     }
 28 |     | 
 29 |     |     /// @custom:oz-upgrades-unsafe-allow constructor
 30 |     |     constructor() initializer {}
 31 |     | 
 32 |     |     function initialize(address admin, address proposer, address sweeper) initializer external {
 33 |     |         // Initialize inheritance chain
 34 |     |         __Ownable_init();
 35 |     |         __UUPSUpgradeable_init();
 36 |     | 
 37 |     |         // Deploy timelock and transfer ownership to it
 38 |     |         transferOwnership(address(new ClimberTimelock(admin, proposer)));
 39 |     | 
 40 |     |         _setSweeper(sweeper);
 41 |     |         _setLastWithdrawal(block.timestamp);
 42 |     |         _lastWithdrawalTimestamp = block.timestamp;
 43 |     |     }
 44 |     | 
 45 |     |     // Allows the owner to send a limited amount of tokens to a recipient every now and then
 46 |     |     function withdraw(address tokenAddress, address recipient, uint256 amount) external onlyOwner {
 47 |     |         require(amount <= WITHDRAWAL_LIMIT, "Withdrawing too much");
 48 |     |         require(block.timestamp > _lastWithdrawalTimestamp + WAITING_PERIOD, "Try later");
 49 |     |         
 50 |     |         _setLastWithdrawal(block.timestamp);
 51 |     | 
 52 |     |         IERC20 token = IERC20(tokenAddress);
 53 |     |         require(token.transfer(recipient, amount), "Transfer failed");
 54 |     |     }
 55 |     | 
 56 |     |     // Allows trusted sweeper account to retrieve any tokens
 57 |     |     function sweepFunds(address tokenAddress) external onlySweeper {
 58 |     |         IERC20 token = IERC20(tokenAddress);
 59 |     |         require(token.transfer(_sweeper, token.balanceOf(address(this))), "Transfer failed");
 60 |     |     }
 61 |     | 
 62 |     |     function getSweeper() external view returns (address) {
 63 |     |         return _sweeper;
 64 |     |     }
 65 |     | 
 66 |     |     function _setSweeper(address newSweeper) internal {
 67 |     |         _sweeper = newSweeper;
 68 |     |     }
 69 |     | 
 70 |     |     function getLastWithdrawalTimestamp() external view returns (uint256) {
 71 |     |         return _lastWithdrawalTimestamp;
 72 |     |     }
 73 |     | 
 74 |     |     function _setLastWithdrawal(uint256 timestamp) internal {
 75 |     |         _lastWithdrawalTimestamp = timestamp;
 76 |     |     }
 77 |     | 
 78 |     |     // By marking this internal function with `onlyOwner`, we only allow the owner account to authorize an upgrade
 79 |     |     function _authorizeUpgrade(address newImplementation) internal onlyOwner override {}
 80 |     | }

  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
  2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Address.sol";
  5 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/security/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
  6 |     | 
  7 |     | import "./TrustfulOracle.sol";

  9 |     | 
 10 |     | /**
 11 |     |  * @title Exchange
 12 |     |  * @author Damn Vulnerable DeFi (https://damnvulnerabledefi.xyz)
 13 |     |  */
 14 |     | contract Exchange is ReentrancyGuard {
 15 |     | 
 16 |     |     using Address for address payable;
 17 |     | 
 18 |     |     DamnValuableNFT public immutable token;
 19 |     |     TrustfulOracle public immutable oracle;
 20 |     | 
 21 |     |     event TokenBought(address indexed buyer, uint256 tokenId, uint256 price);
 22 |     |     event TokenSold(address indexed seller, uint256 tokenId, uint256 price);
 23 |     | 
 24 |     |     constructor(address oracleAddress) payable {
 25 |     |         token = new DamnValuableNFT();
 26 |     |         oracle = TrustfulOracle(oracleAddress);
 27 |     |     }
 28 |     | 
 29 |     |     function buyOne() external payable nonReentrant returns (uint256) {
 30 |     |         uint256 amountPaidInWei = msg.value;
 31 |     |         require(amountPaidInWei > 0, "Amount paid must be greater than zero");
 32 |     | 
 33 |     |         // Price should be in [wei / NFT]
 34 |     |         uint256 currentPriceInWei = oracle.getMedianPrice(token.symbol());
 35 |     |         require(amountPaidInWei >= currentPriceInWei, "Amount paid is not enough");
 36 |     | 
 37 |     |         uint256 tokenId = token.safeMint(msg.sender);
 38 |     |         
 39 |     |         payable(msg.sender).sendValue(amountPaidInWei - currentPriceInWei);
 40 |     | 
 41 |     |         emit TokenBought(msg.sender, tokenId, currentPriceInWei);
 42 |     | 
 43 |     |         return tokenId;
 44 |     |     }
 45 |     | 
 46 |     |     function sellOne(uint256 tokenId) external nonReentrant {
 47 |     |         require(msg.sender == token.ownerOf(tokenId), "Seller must be the owner");
 48 |     |         require(token.getApproved(tokenId) == address(this), "Seller must have approved transfer");
 49 |     | 
 50 |     |         // Price should be in [wei / NFT]
 51 |     |         uint256 currentPriceInWei = oracle.getMedianPrice(token.symbol());
 52 |     |         require(address(this).balance >= currentPriceInWei, "Not enough ETH in balance");
 53 |     | 
 54 |     |         token.transferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), tokenId);
 55 |     |         token.burn(tokenId);
 56 |     |         
 57 |     |         payable(msg.sender).sendValue(currentPriceInWei);
 58 |     | 
 59 |     |         emit TokenSold(msg.sender, tokenId, currentPriceInWei);
 60 |     |     }
 61 |     | 
 62 |     |     receive() external payable {}
 63 |     | }

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/contracts/compromised/TrustfulOracle.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2 |     | 
   3 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
   4 |     | 
   5 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/access/AccessControlEnumerable.sol";
   6 |     | 
   7 |     | /**
   8 |     |  * @title TrustfulOracle
   9 |     |  * @author Damn Vulnerable DeFi (https://damnvulnerabledefi.xyz)
  10 |     |  * @notice A price oracle with a number of trusted sources that individually report prices for symbols.
  11 |     |  *         The oracle's price for a given symbol is the median price of the symbol over all sources.
  12 |     |  */
  13 |     | contract TrustfulOracle is AccessControlEnumerable {
  14 |     | 
  15 |     |     bytes32 public constant TRUSTED_SOURCE_ROLE = keccak256("TRUSTED_SOURCE_ROLE");
  16 |     |     bytes32 public constant INITIALIZER_ROLE = keccak256("INITIALIZER_ROLE");
  17 |     | 
  18 |     |     // Source address => (symbol => price)
  19 |     |     mapping(address => mapping (string => uint256)) private pricesBySource;
  20 |     | 
  21 |     |     modifier onlyTrustedSource() {
  22 |     |         require(hasRole(TRUSTED_SOURCE_ROLE, msg.sender));
  23 |     |         _;
  24 |     |     }
  25 |     | 
  26 |     |     modifier onlyInitializer() {
  27 |     |         require(hasRole(INITIALIZER_ROLE, msg.sender));
  28 |     |         _;
  29 |     |     }
  30 |     | 
  31 |     |     event UpdatedPrice(
  32 |     |         address indexed source,
  33 |     |         string indexed symbol,
  34 |     |         uint256 oldPrice,
  35 |     |         uint256 newPrice
  36 |     |     );
  37 |     | 
  38 |     |     constructor(address[] memory sources, bool enableInitialization) {
  39 |     |         require(sources.length > 0);
  40 |     |         for(uint256 i = 0; i < sources.length; i++) {
  41 |     |             _setupRole(TRUSTED_SOURCE_ROLE, sources[i]);
  42 |     |         }
  43 |     | 
  44 |     |         if (enableInitialization) {
  45 |     |             _setupRole(INITIALIZER_ROLE, msg.sender);
  46 |     |         }
  47 |     |     }
  48 |     | 
  49 |     |     // A handy utility allowing the deployer to setup initial prices (only once)
  50 |     |     function setupInitialPrices(
  51 |     |         address[] memory sources,
  52 |     |         string[] memory symbols,
  53 |     |         uint256[] memory prices
  54 |     |     ) 
  55 |     |         public
  56 |     |         onlyInitializer
  57 |     |     {
  58 |     |         // Only allow one (symbol, price) per source
  59 |     |         require(sources.length == symbols.length && symbols.length == prices.length);
  60 |     |         for(uint256 i = 0; i < sources.length; i++) {
  61 |     |             _setPrice(sources[i], symbols[i], prices[i]);
  62 |     |         }
  63 |     |         renounceRole(INITIALIZER_ROLE, msg.sender);
  64 |     |     }
  65 |     | 
  66 |     |     function postPrice(string calldata symbol, uint256 newPrice) external onlyTrustedSource {
  67 |     |         _setPrice(msg.sender, symbol, newPrice);
  68 |     |     }
  69 |     | 
  70 |     |     function getMedianPrice(string calldata symbol) external view returns (uint256) {
  71 |     |         return _computeMedianPrice(symbol);
  72 |     |     }
  73 |     | 
  74 |     |     function getAllPricesForSymbol(string memory symbol) public view returns (uint256[] memory) {
  75 |     |         uint256 numberOfSources = getNumberOfSources();
  76 |     |         uint256[] memory prices = new uint256[](numberOfSources);
  77 |     | 
  78 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < numberOfSources; i++) {
  79 |     |             address source = getRoleMember(TRUSTED_SOURCE_ROLE, i);
  80 |     |             prices[i] = getPriceBySource(symbol, source);
  81 |     |         }
  82 |     | 
  83 |     |         return prices;
  84 |     |     }
  85 |     | 
  86 |     |     function getPriceBySource(string memory symbol, address source) public view returns (uint256) {
  87 |     |         return pricesBySource[source][symbol];
  88 |     |     }
  89 |     | 
  90 |     |     function getNumberOfSources() public view returns (uint256) {
  91 |     |         return getRoleMemberCount(TRUSTED_SOURCE_ROLE);
  92 |     |     }
  93 |     | 
  94 |     |     function _setPrice(address source, string memory symbol, uint256 newPrice) private {
  95 |     |         uint256 oldPrice = pricesBySource[source][symbol];
  96 |     |         pricesBySource[source][symbol] = newPrice;
  97 |     |         emit UpdatedPrice(source, symbol, oldPrice, newPrice);
  98 |     |     }
  99 |     | 
 100 |     |     function _computeMedianPrice(string memory symbol) private view returns (uint256) {
 101 |     |         uint256[] memory prices = _sort(getAllPricesForSymbol(symbol));
 102 |     | 
 103 |     |         // calculate median price
 104 |     |         if (prices.length % 2 == 0) {
 105 |     |             uint256 leftPrice = prices[(prices.length / 2) - 1];
 106 |     |             uint256 rightPrice = prices[prices.length / 2];
 107 |     |             return (leftPrice + rightPrice) / 2;
 108 |     |         } else {
 109 |     |             return prices[prices.length / 2];
 110 |     |         }
 111 |     |     }
 112 |     | 
 113 |     |     function _sort(uint256[] memory arrayOfNumbers) private pure returns (uint256[] memory) {
 114 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < arrayOfNumbers.length; i++) {
 115 |     |             for (uint256 j = i + 1; j < arrayOfNumbers.length; j++) {
 116 |     |                 if (arrayOfNumbers[i] > arrayOfNumbers[j]) {
 117 |     |                     uint256 tmp = arrayOfNumbers[i];
 118 |     |                     arrayOfNumbers[i] = arrayOfNumbers[j];
 119 |     |                     arrayOfNumbers[j] = tmp;
 120 |     |                 }
 121 |     |             }
 122 |     |         }        
 123 |     |         return arrayOfNumbers;
 124 |     |     }
 125 |     | }

 12 |     | contract FreeRiderNFTMarketplace is ReentrancyGuard {
 13 |     | 
 14 |     |     using Address for address payable;
 15 |     | 
 16 |     |     DamnValuableNFT public token;

 18 |     | 
 19 |     |     // tokenId -> price
 20 |     |     mapping(uint256 => uint256) private offers;
 21 |     | 
 22 |     |     event NFTOffered(address indexed offerer, uint256 tokenId, uint256 price);
 23 |     |     event NFTBought(address indexed buyer, uint256 tokenId, uint256 price);
 24 |     |     
 25 |     |     constructor(uint8 amountToMint) payable {
 26 |     |         require(amountToMint < 256, "Cannot mint that many tokens");
 27 |     |         token = new DamnValuableNFT();
 28 |     | 
 29 |     |         for(uint8 i = 0; i < amountToMint; i++) {
 30 |     |             token.safeMint(msg.sender);
 31 |     |         }        
 32 |     |     }
 33 |     | 
 34 |     |     function offerMany(uint256[] calldata tokenIds, uint256[] calldata prices) external nonReentrant {
 35 |     |         require(tokenIds.length > 0 && tokenIds.length == prices.length);
 36 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < tokenIds.length; i++) {
 37 |     |             _offerOne(tokenIds[i], prices[i]);
 38 |     |         }
 39 |     |     }
 40 |     | 
 41 |     |     function _offerOne(uint256 tokenId, uint256 price) private {
 42 |     |         require(price > 0, "Price must be greater than zero");
 43 |     | 
 44 |     |         require(
 45 |     |             msg.sender == token.ownerOf(tokenId),
 46 |     |             "Account offering must be the owner"
 47 |     |         );
 48 |     | 
 49 |     |         require(
 50 |     |             token.getApproved(tokenId) == address(this) ||
 51 |     |             token.isApprovedForAll(msg.sender, address(this)),
 52 |     |             "Account offering must have approved transfer"
 53 |     |         );
 54 |     | 
 55 |     |         offers[tokenId] = price;
 56 |     | 
 57 |     |         amountOfOffers++;
 58 |     | 
 59 |     |         emit NFTOffered(msg.sender, tokenId, price);
 60 |     |     }
 61 |     | 
 62 |     |     function buyMany(uint256[] calldata tokenIds) external payable nonReentrant {
 63 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < tokenIds.length; i++) {
 64 |     |             _buyOne(tokenIds[i]);
 65 |     |         }
 66 |     |     }
 67 |     | 
 68 |     |     function _buyOne(uint256 tokenId) private {       
 69 |     |         uint256 priceToPay = offers[tokenId];
 70 |     |         require(priceToPay > 0, "Token is not being offered");
 71 |     | 
 72 |     |         require(msg.value >= priceToPay, "Amount paid is not enough");
 73 |     | 
 74 |     |         amountOfOffers--;
 75 |     | 
 76 |     |         // transfer from seller to buyer
 77 |     |         token.safeTransferFrom(token.ownerOf(tokenId), msg.sender, tokenId);
 78 |     | 
 79 |     |         // pay seller
 80 |     |         payable(token.ownerOf(tokenId)).sendValue(priceToPay);
 81 |     | 
 82 |     |         emit NFTBought(msg.sender, tokenId, priceToPay);
 83 |     |     }    
 84 |     | 
 85 |     |     receive() external payable {}
 86 |     | }

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/contracts/puppet/PuppetPool.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
  2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/security/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
  5 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Address.sol";
  6 |     | import "../DamnValuableToken.sol";
  7 |     | 
  8 |     | /**
  9 |     |  * @title PuppetPool
 10 |     |  * @author Damn Vulnerable DeFi (https://damnvulnerabledefi.xyz)
 11 |     |  */
 12 |     | contract PuppetPool is ReentrancyGuard {
 13 |     | 
 14 |     |     using Address for address payable;
 15 |     | 
 16 |     |     mapping(address => uint256) public deposits;
 17 |     |     address public immutable uniswapPair;
 18 |     |     DamnValuableToken public immutable token;
 19 |     |     
 20 |     |     event Borrowed(address indexed account, uint256 depositRequired, uint256 borrowAmount);
 21 |     | 
 22 |     |     constructor (address tokenAddress, address uniswapPairAddress) {
 23 |     |         token = DamnValuableToken(tokenAddress);
 24 |     |         uniswapPair = uniswapPairAddress;
 25 |     |     }
 26 |     | 
 27 |     |     // Allows borrowing `borrowAmount` of tokens by first depositing two times their value in ETH
 28 |     |     function borrow(uint256 borrowAmount) public payable nonReentrant {
 29 |     |         uint256 depositRequired = calculateDepositRequired(borrowAmount);
 30 |     |         
 31 |     |         require(msg.value >= depositRequired, "Not depositing enough collateral");
 32 |     |         
 33 |     |         if (msg.value > depositRequired) {
 34 |     |             payable(msg.sender).sendValue(msg.value - depositRequired);
 35 |     |         }
 36 |     | 
 37 |     |         deposits[msg.sender] = deposits[msg.sender] + depositRequired;
 38 |     | 
 39 |     |         // Fails if the pool doesn't have enough tokens in liquidity
 40 |     |         require(token.transfer(msg.sender, borrowAmount), "Transfer failed");
 41 |     | 
 42 |     |         emit Borrowed(msg.sender, depositRequired, borrowAmount);
 43 |     |     }
 44 |     | 
 45 |     |     function calculateDepositRequired(uint256 amount) public view returns (uint256) {
 46 |     |         return amount * _computeOraclePrice() * 2 / 10 ** 18;
 47 |     |     }
 48 |     | 
 49 |     |     function _computeOraclePrice() private view returns (uint256) {
 50 |     |         // calculates the price of the token in wei according to Uniswap pair
 51 |     |         return uniswapPair.balance * (10 ** 18) / token.balanceOf(uniswapPair);
 52 |     |     }
 53 |     | 
 54 |     |      /**
 55 |     |      ... functions to deposit, redeem, repay, calculate interest, and so on ...
 56 |     |      */
 57 |     | 
 58 |     | }
 59 |     |

/Users/thelostone-mc/Labs/damn-vulnerable-defi-echidna/contracts/selfie/SimpleGovernance.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
  2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.0;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | import "../DamnValuableTokenSnapshot.sol";
  5 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Address.sol";
  6 |     | 
  7 |     | /**
  8 |     |  * @title SimpleGovernance
  9 |     |  * @author Damn Vulnerable DeFi (https://damnvulnerabledefi.xyz)
 10 |     |  */
 11 |     | contract SimpleGovernance {
 12 |     | 
 13 |     |     using Address for address;
 14 |     |     
 15 |     |     struct GovernanceAction {
 16 |     |         address receiver;
 17 |     |         bytes data;
 18 |     |         uint256 weiAmount;
 19 |     |         uint256 proposedAt;
 20 |     |         uint256 executedAt;
 21 |     |     }
 22 |     |     
 23 |     |     DamnValuableTokenSnapshot public governanceToken;
 24 |     | 
 25 |     |     mapping(uint256 => GovernanceAction) public actions;
 26 |     |     uint256 private actionCounter;
 27 |     |     uint256 private ACTION_DELAY_IN_SECONDS = 2 days;
 28 |     | 
 29 |     |     event ActionQueued(uint256 actionId, address indexed caller);
 30 |     |     event ActionExecuted(uint256 actionId, address indexed caller);
 31 |     | 
 32 |     |     constructor(address governanceTokenAddress) {
 33 |     |         require(governanceTokenAddress != address(0), "Governance token cannot be zero address");
 34 |     |         governanceToken = DamnValuableTokenSnapshot(governanceTokenAddress);
 35 |     |         actionCounter = 1;
 36 |     |     }
 37 |     |     
 38 |     |     function queueAction(address receiver, bytes calldata data, uint256 weiAmount) external returns (uint256) {
 39 |     |         require(_hasEnoughVotes(msg.sender), "Not enough votes to propose an action");
 40 |     |         require(receiver != address(this), "Cannot queue actions that affect Governance");
 41 |     | 
 42 |     |         uint256 actionId = actionCounter;
 43 |     | 
 44 |     |         GovernanceAction storage actionToQueue = actions[actionId];
 45 |     |         actionToQueue.receiver = receiver;
 46 |     |         actionToQueue.weiAmount = weiAmount;
 47 |     |         actionToQueue.data = data;
 48 |     |         actionToQueue.proposedAt = block.timestamp;
 49 |     | 
 50 |     |         actionCounter++;
 51 |     | 
 52 |     |         emit ActionQueued(actionId, msg.sender);
 53 |     |         return actionId;
 54 |     |     }
 55 |     | 
 56 |     |     function executeAction(uint256 actionId) external payable {
 57 |     |         require(_canBeExecuted(actionId), "Cannot execute this action");
 58 |     |         
 59 |     |         GovernanceAction storage actionToExecute = actions[actionId];
 60 |     |         actionToExecute.executedAt = block.timestamp;
 61 |     | 
 62 |     |         actionToExecute.receiver.functionCallWithValue(
 63 |     |             actionToExecute.data,
 64 |     |             actionToExecute.weiAmount
 65 |     |         );
 66 |     | 
 67 |     |         emit ActionExecuted(actionId, msg.sender);
 68 |     |     }
 69 |     | 
 70 |     |     function getActionDelay() public view returns (uint256) {
 71 |     |         return ACTION_DELAY_IN_SECONDS;
 72 |     |     }
 73 |     | 
 74 |     |     /**
 75 |     |      * @dev an action can only be executed if:
 76 |     |      * 1) it's never been executed before and
 77 |     |      * 2) enough time has passed since it was first proposed
 78 |     |      */
 79 |     |     function _canBeExecuted(uint256 actionId) private view returns (bool) {
 80 |     |         GovernanceAction memory actionToExecute = actions[actionId];
 81 |     |         return (
 82 |     |             actionToExecute.executedAt == 0 &&
 83 |     |             (block.timestamp - actionToExecute.proposedAt >= ACTION_DELAY_IN_SECONDS)
 84 |     |         );
 85 |     |     }
 86 |     |     
 87 |     |     function _hasEnoughVotes(address account) private view returns (bool) {
 88 |     |         uint256 balance = governanceToken.getBalanceAtLastSnapshot(account);
 89 |     |         uint256 halfTotalSupply = governanceToken.getTotalSupplyAtLastSnapshot() / 2;
 90 |     |         return balance > halfTotalSupply;
 91 |     |     }
 92 |     | }

 12 |     |  *         with snapshotting capabilities

 34 |     |         _burn(from, amount);
 35 |     |     }

 40 |     |     }
